Zhenjiu Zisheng Jing针灸资生经

Chapter 6卷5

Original · plain Chinese · English · notes原文 · 白话 · 英文 · 学习要点

Classical texts医书对照 › Zhenjiu Zisheng Jing针灸资生经 › Chapter 6卷5

type=子欽定四庫全書

《四库全书》子部钦定本的版本标识。

This is the edition designation indicating that the text belongs to the imperially commissioned Siku Quanshu, classified under the Zi division.

💡 子部收录诸子、医学、艺术及其他专门著作。此行属版本与目录资讯，不是医学正文。

鍼灸資生經卷五

《针灸资生经》第五卷。

The fifth volume of the Classic of Supporting Life through Acupuncture and Moxibustion.

💡 《针灸资生经》为南宋王执中编撰的针灸文献，广泛辑录《铜人腧穴针灸图经》《明堂经》《千金方》等前代医籍。阅读时须区分王执中的按语与所引古籍内容。

臍痛

脐部疼痛。

Pain around the umbilicus.

💡 本篇讨论脐周、脐上及脐下疼痛，以及古代针灸文献对相关症候的辨证和取穴。现代临床须先区分胃肠、泌尿、生殖、腹壁及血管等原因。

中極療臍下塊如覆杯明見杯銅云結如覆杯見杯闗元治臍下㽲痛小便赤澁不覺遺瀝小便處痛狀如散火溺血3093疝痛臍下結血狀如覆杯明同轉胞不得銅隂交治臍下㽲痛女子月事不絶帶下產後惡露不止繞臍冷痛中封見瘧水分見水腫神闕見瀉治繞臍痛曲泉主痛引臍中千見疝

中极穴主治脐下出现如倒扣杯子般的肿块。《明堂》记载其主治脐下肿块，形状如覆杯；《铜人》也记载结块如覆杯。关元穴治疗脐下急迫性疼痛、小便赤色而涩滞、排尿时不能察觉尿液滴沥，以及尿道部位疼痛如火散开、小便出血、突发疝痛（原文“3093”为底本缺字“暴”）、脐下血结成块如覆杯等症。《明堂》亦有相同记载。另记载转胞、排尿困难。《铜人》记载，阴交穴治疗脐下急迫性疼痛、女性月经不断、带下、产后恶露不止，以及绕脐冷痛。中封穴的主治参见疟疾篇；水分穴的主治参见水肿篇；神阙穴的主治参见泄泻篇。神阙穴治疗绕脐疼痛。曲泉穴主治疼痛牵引至脐中。《千金方》相关内容参见疝病篇。

Zhongji is indicated for a mass below the umbilicus resembling an inverted cup. The Ming Tang describes a lump beneath the umbilicus with the shape of an overturned cup, and the Tong Ren records a similar description. Guanyuan treats acute, cramping pain below the umbilicus, reddish and difficult urination, unrecognized urinary dribbling, pain at the urinary outlet resembling spreading fire, bloody urine, hernial pain, and a blood-like mass below the umbilicus resembling an overturned cup. The Ming Tang gives similar indications. The text also mentions urinary retention attributed to turning of the bladder. The Tong Ren states that Yinjiao treats acute pain below the umbilicus, prolonged menstruation, vaginal discharge, persistent postpartum lochia, and cold pain around the umbilicus. Zhongfeng is discussed under malaria, Shuifen under edema, and Shenque under diarrhea. Shenque is also indicated for pain encircling the umbilicus. Ququan is indicated for pain radiating toward the umbilicus. The related discussion in the Qian Jin Fang appears under hernia.

💡 穴位代码：中极 CV3；关元 CV4；阴交 CV7；中封 LR4；水分 CV9；神阙 CV8；曲泉 LR8。『覆杯』是古代对腹部肿块形态的描述，不能直接等同于某种现代肿瘤或囊肿。『㽲痛』指急迫、剧烈或阵发性疼痛。『遗沥』指排尿后滴沥或尿液不自主漏出；『转胞』是古代描述排尿困难的一种病名。这些症状在现代可能涉及尿路感染、结石、尿潴留、盆腔疾病等，需要检查。经穴主治属传统文献记载，不能据此诊断或保证疗效。

予舊苦臍中疼則欲溏㵼常以手中指按之少止或正㵼下亦按之則不疼它日灸臍中遂不疼矣後义嘗溏利不已灸之則止凡臍疼者宜灸神闕

我从前常受脐中疼痛困扰，每当疼痛发作，便有想要排稀便、腹泻的感觉。常用中指按压脐部，疼痛便稍稍缓解；即使正在腹泻，按压脐部也能使疼痛消失。后来有一天，我在脐中施灸，从此疼痛便不再发作。再后来，又曾经持续排稀便，施灸后便停止了。因此，凡是脐部疼痛，可以考虑灸神阙穴。

I formerly suffered from pain at the center of my umbilicus. When the pain occurred, I felt an urge to pass loose stools or develop diarrhea. Pressing the umbilical region with my middle finger usually relieved the pain somewhat. Even while diarrhea was occurring, pressing the area made the pain disappear. On another occasion, I applied moxibustion to the umbilical region, after which the pain ceased. Later, when I again experienced persistent loose stools, moxibustion stopped them. Thus, for umbilical pain, the text recommends moxibustion at Shenque.

💡 穴位：神阙 CV8。这是王执中记录自己的治疗经验，属个案观察，不是对所有脐痛或腹泻患者均有效的临床证据。中指按压后疼痛减轻，可能涉及腹壁感觉刺激、注意力调节或疼痛调节机制。现代研究中的穴位按压与灸法效果须分别评估。神阙位于脐部，施灸时尤其须避免烫伤、感染及腹壁损伤，不应自行在脐内填入艾炷或直接施以高温灸。持续腹泻、剧烈腹痛、血便、发热或脱水应及时就医。

闗門治氣遊走夾臍急見積氣下腕治臍上厥氣動見腹痛氣海治臍下冷氣上衝心血結成塊狀如覆杯小便赤澁腹結治繞臍痛上搶心千同腹寒洩利欬逆天樞治夾臍切痛時上衝心煩滿嘔吐霍亂明下云冷氣臍痛見冷氣千云冬感寒臍痛見㵼外陵主心如懸下引臍腹痛千見腹痛上㢘治夾臍腹痛見脅痛四滿治歴下切痛見積聚分水療水腫繞臍痛明下小兒臍腫灸腰後對臍骨節閒三壯然谷主兒臍風口不開銅同善驚遶臍絞痛灸天樞百壯千見癥瘕臍下絞痛灸闗元百壯見腹寒熱臍中石門等主疝繞臍臍中石門天樞主臍疝遶臍蠡溝療臍中積氣千並見疝

关门穴治疗气在腹中游走、脐旁急迫疼痛，相关内容参见积气篇。下脘穴治疗脐上厥逆之气上冲，相关内容参见腹痛篇。气海穴治疗脐下寒气上冲心胸、血结成如覆杯状的肿块、小便赤涩及腹部结聚。腹结穴治疗绕脐疼痛、向上冲击心胸的疼痛；《千金方》另记载其主治腹部寒冷、泄泻及咳逆。天枢穴治疗脐旁剧烈疼痛，有时向上冲击心胸，伴随心烦、腹部胀满、呕吐及霍乱。《明堂》下卷称其治疗冷气所致的脐痛；《千金方》记载冬季感受寒邪而发生脐痛，相关内容参见泄泻篇。外陵穴主治心中如悬空、疼痛向下牵引至脐腹。《千金方》相关内容参见腹痛篇。上廉穴治疗脐旁腹痛，参见胁痛篇。四满穴治疗脐下部位的剧烈疼痛，参见积聚篇。分水穴治疗水肿及绕脐疼痛。《明堂》下卷记载，小儿脐部肿胀，可以在腰后正对脐部的脊椎骨节间施灸三壮。然谷穴主治小儿脐风、口不能张开，《铜人》记载亦相同，并主治容易受惊以及绕脐绞痛。脐部绞痛，可灸天枢穴一百壮；《千金方》相关内容参见癥瘕篇。脐下绞痛，可灸关元穴一百壮，相关内容参见腹部寒热篇。脐中、石门等穴主治绕脐疝气。脐中、石门、天枢主治脐疝及绕脐疼痛。蠡沟穴治疗脐中积气，《千金方》相关内容亦参见疝病篇。

Guanmen treats wandering abdominal qi with acute pain beside the umbilicus; see the section on accumulated qi. Xiawan treats rebellious qi moving upward above the umbilicus; see the section on abdominal pain. Qihai treats cold qi below the umbilicus surging toward the heart, blood-like accumulations forming a mass resembling an overturned cup, reddish and difficult urination, and abdominal masses. Fujie treats pain around the umbilicus with upward surging toward the chest and heart. The Qian Jin Fang also lists abdominal cold, diarrhea, and cough with rebellious qi. Tianshu treats severe pain beside the umbilicus that sometimes surges upward toward the heart, accompanied by restlessness, fullness, vomiting, and cholera-like disorders. The lower volume of the Ming Tang attributes umbilical pain to cold qi, while the Qian Jin Fang describes umbilical pain after exposure to winter cold; see the diarrhea section. Wailing is indicated for a sensation of suspension in the chest or heart with pain pulling downward into the umbilical abdomen. See the abdominal pain section of the Qian Jin Fang. Shanglian treats pain beside the umbilicus; see the section on hypochondriac pain. Siman treats severe pain below the umbilicus; see the section on accumulations. Shuifen treats edema and pain around the umbilicus. The lower Ming Tang states that swelling of an infant's umbilicus may be treated by applying three moxa cones between the vertebral joints of the lower back directly opposite the umbilicus. Rangu is indicated for infantile umbilical wind with inability to open the mouth. The Tong Ren gives similar indications and also mentions susceptibility to fright and colicky pain around the umbilicus. For colicky umbilical pain, the text recommends one hundred moxa cones at Tianshu. See the Qian Jin Fang section on abdominal masses. For severe pain below the umbilicus, it recommends one hundred moxa cones at Guanyuan; see the section on abdominal cold and heat. The umbilicus and Shimen, among other points, are listed for hernia involving the umbilical region. The umbilicus, Shimen, and Tianshu are listed for umbilical hernia and pain around the umbilicus. Ligou treats accumulated qi at the umbilicus; the Qian Jin Fang also discusses this under hernia.

💡 穴位代码：关门 ST22；下脘 CV10；气海 CV6；腹结 SP14；天枢 ST25；外陵 ST26；上廉 LI9；四满 KI14；分水 CV9；然谷 KI2；关元 CV4；石门 CV5；蠡沟 LR5。『气游走』、『冷气』、『厥气』是传统气机病理描述，不能直接视为现代解剖学中的气体移动。『霍乱』在古籍中可泛指突然发作的剧烈吐泻，不宜直接等同于现代霍乱弧菌感染。『脐风』是古代儿科病名，与新生儿破伤风等疾病的历史描述可能存在交集，但不能简单画等号。『百壮』指艾炷灸的数量，是古代操作记录，不是现代安全施灸剂量。小儿脐肿、口不能开、惊厥或剧烈腹痛均属需要及时医疗评估的情况。

膺痛

胸膺部疼痛。

Pain in the anterior chest, particularly the pectoral region.

💡 『膺』指胸前部，尤其是胸壁及乳房周围。古代膺痛的范围比现代某一个单独诊断更广。

玉堂療胷滿膺痛見心煩玉堂治胷膺骨疼紫宫治胷膺骨疼大谿治乳腫賁膺並見胷脅痛

玉堂穴治疗胸部胀满及胸膺疼痛，相关内容参见心烦篇。《玉堂》条又记载，玉堂穴治疗胸膺骨疼痛。紫宫穴也治疗胸膺骨疼痛。大溪穴治疗乳房肿胀及胸膺部位突起或胀满，相关内容一并参见胸胁痛篇。

Yutang treats fullness of the chest and pectoral pain; see the section on restlessness of the heart. The Yutang entry also states that it treats pain in the bones of the anterior chest. Zigong likewise treats pain in the anterior chest bones. Taixi treats breast swelling and fullness or prominence of the pectoral region; see also the section on chest and hypochondriac pain.

💡 穴位代码：玉堂 CV18；紫宫 CV19；大溪 KI3。『贲膺』描述胸前隆起或胀满。胸骨、肋软骨、胸壁及乳房疼痛的病因不同，现代应区分肌肉骨骼问题、乳腺疾病、心肺疾病等。尤其新发胸痛伴胸闷、呼吸困难、冷汗或放射痛，应优先排除急性心血管疾病。

胷滿胷脅滿1841胷

胸满、胸胁满、龟胸。

Chest fullness, fullness of the chest and flanks, and pigeon chest (guixiong).

💡 『胸满』是症状描述，可涉及胸闷、胸部压迫感、胀满或呼吸不畅。原文“1841胷”的1841为底本缺字“龟”（本书“背偻如1841”“尾1841骨”“1841尾”同此），“龟胸”即胸骨向前突起的畸形，本节末条专论小儿龟胸。

凡胷滿短氣不得汗皆鍼補手太隂以出汗千又見霍亂神堂主胷腹滿三閒主胷滿腸鳴陽谿天容主胷滿不得息曲池人迎神道章門中府明下同臨泣天池璇璣府俞主胷中滿陽交主胷滿腫鳩尾主胷滿欬逆大泉主胷滿噭呼胷膺痛巨闕閒使主胷中淡淡梁門主胷下積氣期門缺盆主胷中熱息賁脇下氣上雲門主胷中暴逆心俞大杼主胷中鬰鬰然谷主胷中寒脉代時不至寸口小腹脹上搶心胷脅滿灸期門見心痛玉堂見心煩療胷滿膺痛明三閒療胷滿腹鳴亶中見肺癰療胷中氣滿如塞乳根療胷下滿悶陽交見療痛臨泣見腋腫治胷滿委陽治胷滿膨膨銅見尸厥璇璣治胷皮滿痛明同下云胷脅支滿俞府治胷滿見喘商陽治胷中氣滿喘欬支腫鬲闗治胷中噎悶見背痛陽谿神封治胷滿不得息欬逆肺俞治胷中氣滿背僂如1841腰强頭目3392令人失顏色輙筋治胷中3093滿不得卧喘息鬲俞治胷滿支腫見嘔吐胃俞治胷脅支滿見腹脹涌泉神堂治胷腹滿見肩痛中庭治胷脇支滿噎塞食不下嘔吐食還出雲門治喉痹胷煩滿氣上衝心欬喘不得息胷脇短氣肩痛不得舉臂天池治胷中有聲見鬲病曲池治胷中煩滿胷鄉治胷脅滿引胷背痛不得明下有卧字轉側周榮治胷脅滿不得俛仰食不下欬唾稠膿明下同彧中治胷脇支滿欬逆喘不能食明同神藏治胷脇支滿欬逆喘不得息嘔吐胷滿不嗜食明同氣户治胷脇支滿喘逆上氣胷背急不得息不知食味食竇治胷脇支滿膈閒雷鳴常有水聲靈墟治胷脇支滿痛引胷不得息欬逆嘔吐胷滿不嗜食明同步郎治胷脇支滿鼻塞不通呼吸少氣喘息不得舉臂明同章門治胷脇支滿見腸鳴庫房治胷脇支滿欬逆上氣多唾濁沫膿血期門治產後胷脇支滿見心痛外丘治胷脇脹滿俠谿治胷脇支滿寒熱汗不出雲門療胷脇徹背痛見上氣華盖療胷脇滿痛引胷明見喘紫宫見心煩中庭見反胃涌泉治胷脇支滿通谷章門曲泉鬲俞期門食竇明所陷谷石門主胷脇支滿千胃俞三里紫宫華盖中庭神藏靈墟俠谿步郎商陽上廉氣户周榮上管勞官涌泉陽陵泉主胷脇注滿膽俞明下同章門主脇痛不得卧胷滿歐無所出前谷主欬而胷滿陽氣逆上滿胷取天容見上氣肺俞巨闕主胷滿見上氣太泉治胷脇支滿銅見心痛天髎治胷中煩悶見肩背肝俞療兩脇滿明見欬浮白療胷滿庫房療胷腹兩脇滿見欬天池治寒熱胷鬲煩滿頭痛四支不舉腋下腫上氣胷中有聲喉鳴膺1495治胷滿短氣魂門療胷背痛見尸厥小兒1841胷縁肺熱脹滿攻胷鬲所生又縁乳母食熱麵五辛胷轉起髙灸兩乳前各寸半上兩行三骨罅閒六處各三壯春夏從下灸上秋冬從上灸下若不依此法灸十不愈一二

凡是胸部胀满、气短而且不能出汗的情况，古籍主张针刺手太阴经穴，采用补法以促使出汗。《千金方》相关内容亦见霍乱篇。神堂穴主治胸腹胀满。三间穴主治胸满及肠鸣。阳溪穴、天容穴主治胸满、呼吸不畅。曲池、人迎、神道、章门、中府等穴，《明堂》下卷记载亦相同；临泣、天池、璇玑、府俞等穴主治胸中胀满。阳交穴主治胸满肿胀。鸠尾穴主治胸满、咳嗽及气逆。大泉（当即太泉，太渊的避讳名）主治胸满、呼号、胸膺疼痛。巨阙、间使主治胸中淡淡（隐隐不适）。梁门穴主治胸下积气。期门、缺盆主治胸中发热及息贲、胁下之气向上冲。云门穴主治胸中气突然逆乱上冲。心俞、大杼主治胸中郁闷。然谷主治胸中寒冷。脉搏间歇、寸口脉有时不能触及、小腹胀满而气向上冲击心胸，以及胸胁胀满等症，可灸期门穴，相关内容参见心痛篇。玉堂穴相关内容参见心烦篇，亦治疗胸满及膺痛。《明堂》记载，三间穴治疗胸满、腹鸣。膻中穴相关内容参见肺痈篇，治疗胸中气满如有东西堵塞。乳根穴治疗胸下胀满不适。阳交穴的主治参见疼痛篇。临泣穴相关内容参见腋肿篇，亦治胸满。委阳穴治疗胸部膨胀、胀满。《铜人》相关内容参见尸厥篇。璇玑穴治疗胸部皮肤胀满疼痛，《明堂》记载相同；下卷又记载其主治胸胁支撑胀满。俞府穴治疗胸满，相关内容参见喘证篇。商阳穴治疗胸中气满、喘咳及肢体浮肿。膈关穴治疗胸中噎塞闷堵，相关内容参见背痛篇。阳溪、神封治疗胸满、呼吸不畅及咳嗽气逆。肺俞穴治疗胸中气满、背部弯曲如龟（驼背）、腰部强直、头目眩晕、使人失去面色光泽（原文“1841”“3392”为底本缺字“龟”“眩”）。辄筋穴治疗胸中暴满（原文“3093”为底本缺字“暴”）、不能平卧及喘息。膈俞治疗胸满、肢体肿胀，相关内容参见呕吐篇。胃俞治疗胸胁支撑胀满，相关内容参见腹胀篇。涌泉、神堂治疗胸腹胀满，相关内容参见肩痛篇。中庭穴治疗胸胁支撑胀满、咽食阻塞、食物不能下咽、呕吐以及食物入口后又吐出。云门穴治疗喉痹、胸中烦闷胀满、气向上冲击心胸、咳嗽气喘、呼吸不畅、胸胁气短，以及肩痛、手臂不能抬举。天池穴治疗胸中有声响，相关内容参见膈病篇。曲池穴治疗胸中烦闷胀满。胸乡穴治疗胸胁胀满，疼痛牵引至胸背，不能自在翻身；《明堂》下卷另有『卧』字。周荣穴治疗胸胁胀满、不能俯仰、进食困难，以及咳嗽、咳吐黏稠脓痰，《明堂》记载相同。彧中穴治疗胸胁支撑胀满、咳逆、气喘及不能进食，《明堂》记载相同。神藏穴治疗胸胁支撑胀满、咳逆、气喘、呼吸不畅、呕吐、胸满及食欲不振，《明堂》记载相同。气户穴治疗胸胁支撑胀满、喘逆上气、胸背拘急、呼吸困难及食物味觉不敏。食窦穴治疗胸胁支撑胀满、膈间雷鸣及常有水声。灵墟穴治疗胸胁支撑胀满疼痛、疼痛牵引胸部、呼吸不畅、咳逆、呕吐、胸满及食欲不振，《明堂》记载相同。步廊穴治疗胸胁支撑胀满、鼻塞不通、呼吸短促、喘息及手臂不能抬举，《明堂》记载相同。章门穴治疗胸胁支撑胀满，相关内容参见肠鸣篇。库房穴治疗胸胁支撑胀满、咳逆上气，以及大量咳吐混浊泡沫、脓液和血液。期门穴治疗产后胸胁支撑胀满，相关内容参见心痛篇。外丘穴治疗胸胁胀满。侠溪穴治疗胸胁支撑胀满、寒热交替及无汗。云门穴治疗胸胁疼痛并贯穿至背部，相关内容参见上气篇。华盖穴治疗胸胁胀满疼痛并牵引胸部，《明堂》相关内容参见喘证篇。紫宫穴相关内容参见心烦篇。中庭穴相关内容参见反胃篇。涌泉穴治疗胸胁支撑胀满。通谷、章门、曲泉、膈俞、期门、食窦等穴，《明堂》记载主治胸胁支撑胀满；陷谷、石门亦有相关主治。《千金方》记载，胃俞、足三里、紫宫、华盖、中庭、神藏、灵墟、侠溪、步廊、商阳、上廉、气户、周荣、上脘、劳宫、涌泉、阳陵泉等穴主治胸胁气机壅滞、胀满。胆俞穴，《明堂》下卷记载相同。章门穴主治胁痛、不能平卧、胸满及呕吐而无物可吐。前谷穴主治咳嗽、胸满及阳气向上逆行。天容穴相关内容参见上气篇。肺俞、巨阙主治胸满，相关内容参见上气篇。太泉穴治疗胸胁支撑胀满，《铜人》相关内容参见心痛篇。天髎穴治疗胸中烦闷，相关内容参见肩背篇。肝俞穴治疗两胁胀满，《明堂》相关内容参见咳嗽篇。浮白穴治疗胸满，《明堂》相关内容参见咳嗽篇。库房穴治疗胸、腹及两胁胀满，相关内容参见咳嗽篇。天池穴治疗寒热、胸膈烦闷胀满、头痛、四肢不能活动、腋下肿胀、上气、胸中有声及喉中鸣响。膺窗（原文“膺1495”，1495为底本缺字“窗”）治疗胸满、气短。魂门穴治疗胸背疼痛，相关内容参见尸厥篇。小儿龟胸（原文“1841胷”，胸骨前凸），是肺热胀满、攻冲胸膈所致，也可能与乳母食用热性面食及五辛有关。古法主张在两乳前方各一寸半处、上方两行、三根骨间隙中的六个位置施灸，每处三壮。春夏从下向上施灸，秋冬从上向下施灸。如果不依照此法施灸，十人中可能只有一两人痊愈。

For chest fullness, shortness of breath, and inability to sweat, the text recommends supplementing the Hand Taiyin channel with acupuncture to induce sweating. The Qian Jin Fang also discusses this under cholera-like disorders. Shentang treats fullness of the chest and abdomen. Sanjian treats chest fullness and intestinal rumbling. Yangxi and Tianrong treat chest fullness with difficulty breathing. Quchi, Renying, Shendao, Zhangmen, and Zhongfu are also listed; the lower Ming Tang gives the same information. Linqi, Tianchi, Xuanji, and Fushu are indicated for fullness in the chest. Yangjiao treats swelling and fullness of the chest. Jiuwei treats chest fullness, coughing, and rebellious qi. Daquan (i.e. Taiquan, a taboo-avoidance name of Taiyuan) treats chest fullness with crying out and pain of the breast region. Juque and Jianshi treat vague discomfort in the chest. Liangmen treats accumulated qi below the chest. Qimen and Quepen treat heat in the chest and a condition described as breath-related accumulation, with qi below the ribs rising upward. Yunmen treats sudden upward rebellion in the chest. Xinshu and Dazhu treat a sensation of oppression in the chest. Rangu treats coldness in the chest. The text also lists intermittent pulses, an occasionally absent pulse at the cun position, lower abdominal distension, upward surging toward the heart, and chest and hypochondriac fullness as indications for moxibustion at Qimen; see the section on heart pain. Yutang is discussed under restlessness of the heart and also treats chest fullness and pectoral pain. The Ming Tang states that Sanjian treats chest fullness and intestinal rumbling. Danzhong is discussed under lung abscess and treats fullness in the chest as though something were obstructing it. Rugen treats oppressive fullness below the chest. Yangjiao is discussed under pain, and Linqi under axillary swelling; both are also associated with chest fullness. Weiyang treats marked distension of the chest. See the Tong Ren discussion under corpse syncope. Xuanji treats painful fullness of the chest wall; the lower Ming Tang gives the same indication and additionally mentions distending fullness of the chest and hypochondria. Shufu treats chest fullness; see the section on dyspnea. Shangyang treats fullness of qi in the chest, dyspnea, cough, and swelling of the limbs. Geguan treats obstruction and oppression in the chest; see the section on back pain. Yangxi and Shenfeng treat chest fullness, difficulty breathing, and cough with rebellious qi. Feishu treats fullness of qi in the chest a back hunched like a tortoise, stiff lower back, dizziness of the head and eyes, and loss of facial colour. Zhejin treats chest fullness, inability to lie down, and panting. Geshu treats chest fullness and swelling; see the section on vomiting. Weishu treats distending fullness of the chest and hypochondria; see abdominal distension. Yongquan and Shentang treat fullness of the chest and abdomen; see shoulder pain. Zhongting treats distending fullness of the chest and hypochondria, obstruction of swallowing, inability to swallow food, vomiting, and regurgitation of food. Yunmen treats throat impediment, oppressive fullness of the chest, upward surging of qi toward the heart, cough, dyspnea, inability to breathe comfortably, shortness of breath involving the chest and hypochondria, shoulder pain, and inability to raise the arm. Tianchi treats sounds in the chest; see diseases of the diaphragm. Quchi treats restlessness and fullness in the chest. Xiongxiang treats distension of the chest and hypochondria with pain radiating toward the chest and back, making turning difficult. The lower Ming Tang contains the additional character 'lying down'. Zhourong treats distending fullness of the chest and hypochondria, inability to bend forward or backward, difficulty eating, coughing, and expectoration of thick purulent sputum. The Ming Tang gives the same indications. Yuzhong treats distending fullness of the chest and hypochondria, rebellious coughing, dyspnea, and inability to eat; the Ming Tang agrees. Shencang treats distending fullness of the chest and hypochondria, rebellious coughing, dyspnea, difficulty breathing, vomiting, chest fullness, and poor appetite; the Ming Tang agrees. Qihu treats distending fullness of the chest and hypochondria, upward rebellious qi, tightness of the chest and back, difficulty breathing, and impaired appreciation of food flavors. Shidou treats distending fullness of the chest and hypochondria, rumbling between the diaphragm, and persistent water-like sounds. Lingxu treats painful distending fullness of the chest and hypochondria, pain radiating toward the chest, difficulty breathing, rebellious coughing, vomiting, chest fullness, and poor appetite; the Ming Tang agrees. Bulang treats distending fullness of the chest and hypochondria, nasal obstruction, shallow breathing, dyspnea, and inability to raise the arm; the Ming Tang agrees. Zhangmen treats distending fullness of the chest and hypochondria; see the section on intestinal rumbling. Kufang treats distending fullness of the chest and hypochondria, rebellious coughing and upward qi, and copious expectoration of turbid foam, pus, and blood. Qimen treats postpartum distending fullness of the chest and hypochondria; see the section on heart pain. Waiqiu treats distension of the chest and hypochondria. Xiaxi treats distending fullness of the chest and hypochondria, alternating cold and heat, and absence of sweating. Yunmen treats pain extending from the chest and hypochondria through to the back; see the section on upward qi. Huagai treats fullness and pain of the chest and hypochondria radiating toward the chest; see the dyspnea section in the Ming Tang. Zigong is discussed under restlessness of the heart. Zhongting is discussed under regurgitation. Yongquan treats distending fullness of the chest and hypochondria. Tonggu, Zhangmen, Ququan, Geshu, Qimen, and Shidou are listed by the Ming Tang for distending fullness of the chest and hypochondria. Xian'gu and Shimen are also listed. The Qian Jin Fang lists Weishu, Zusanli, Zigong, Huagai, Zhongting, Shencang, Lingxu, Xiaxi, Bulang, Shangyang, Shanglian, Qihu, Zhourong, Shangwan, Laogong, Yongquan, and Yanglingquan for obstructed and distended qi in the chest and hypochondria. Danshu is similarly listed in the lower Ming Tang. Zhangmen treats hypochondriac pain, inability to lie down, chest fullness, and vomiting without bringing anything up. Qiangu treats cough with chest fullness and upward rebellion of yang qi. Tianrong is discussed under upward qi. Feishu and Juque treat chest fullness; see upward qi. Taiquan treats distending fullness of the chest and hypochondria; see heart pain in the Tong Ren. Tianliao treats oppressive restlessness in the chest; see the shoulder and back section. Ganshu treats fullness of both hypochondria; see coughing in the Ming Tang. Fubai treats chest fullness; see coughing in the Ming Tang. Kufang treats fullness of the chest, abdomen, and both hypochondria; see coughing. Tianchi treats alternating cold and heat, oppressive fullness of the chest and diaphragm, headache, inability to move the four limbs, axillary swelling, upward qi, sounds in the chest, and wheezing in the throat. Yingchuang (ST16) treats chest fullness and shortness of breath. Hunmen treats pain in the chest and back; see corpse syncope. Pigeon chest in infants is attributed to lung heat attacking the chest and diaphragm, and may also be associated with the nursing mother's consumption of hot noodles and pungent foods. The text prescribes moxibustion at six locations in two rows, each one and a half cun in front of the nipples and situated in the spaces between three bones, applying three cones at each location. In spring and summer, moxibustion proceeds from below upward; in autumn and winter, from above downward. It claims that without following this method, only one or two out of ten cases will recover.

💡 主要穴位代码如下：神堂 BL44；三间 LI3；阳溪 LI5；天容 SI17；曲池 LI11；人迎 ST9；神道 GV11；章门 LR13；中府 LU1；临泣 GB41（本段多指足临泣）；天池 PC1；璇玑 CV21；府俞（疑为俞府 KI27 倒文）；膺窗 ST16；阳交 GB35；鸠尾 CV15；大泉（疑为太泉或太渊的异文，不能直接确定）；巨阙 CV14；间使 PC5；梁门 ST21；期门 LR14；缺盆 ST12；云门 LU2；心俞 BL15；大杼 BL11；然谷 KI2；玉堂 CV18；膻中 CV17；乳根 ST18；委阳 BL39；俞府 KI27；商阳 LI1；膈关 BL46；神封 KI23；肺俞 BL13；辄筋 GB23；膈俞 BL17；胃俞 BL21；涌泉 KI1；中庭 CV16；胸乡 SP19；周荣 SP20；彧中 KI26；神藏 KI25；气户 ST13；食窦 SP17；灵墟 KI24；步廊 KI22；外丘 GB36；侠溪 GB43；通谷 KI20（须依上下文辨认穴名）；陷谷 ST43；石门 CV5；足三里 ST36；上廉 LI9；上脘 CV13；劳宫 PC8；阳陵泉 GB34；胆俞 BL19；前谷 SI2；天髎 TE15；肝俞 BL18；浮白 GB10；库房 ST14；魂门 BL47。古籍将『手太阴补法以出汗』作为治疗胸满短气无汗的方法，这是传统经络治疗主张，现代不能将针刺当作诱发出汗或治疗胸闷气短的常规替代疗法。『肺痈』、『息贲』、『上气』、『气逆』等均是古代病候术语，不能直接等同于肺脓肿、肿瘤或某一种呼吸系统疾病。咳出脓血、呼吸困难、不能平卧、胸中堵塞及突发气短，现代均属需要及时评估的警讯。『胸中有水声』、『膈间雷鸣』可与胃肠液体活动等现象作描述性比较，但不能仅凭声音确诊。『春夏从下灸上、秋冬从上灸下』属古代施灸规则，缺乏可直接套用的现代循证依据。原文儿科灸法的位置、壮数和季节方向不应作为家庭操作指南。

胷脇痛胷痹痛餘見胷滿

胸胁疼痛、胸痹疼痛，其余相关内容参见胸满篇。

Pain in the chest and hypochondria, and pain associated with chest impediment. For further information, see the section on chest fullness.

💡 『胸痹』是中医重要病证术语，常指胸部闭塞、疼痛、胸闷及呼吸不畅等表现。传统上与心脉痹阻、胸阳不展等理论相关。现代胸痹症状可能与冠心病、心绞痛、心肌梗死、肺部疾病或食管疾病有关，必须先排除危急病因。

本神顱息主胷脇相引不得傾側千太白主胷脇脹切痛陽輔主胷脇痛環跳至隂主胷脇痛無常處腰脇相引急痛大包主胷脇中痛豐隆又見腹痛丘墟主胷痛如刺胷脇滿心痛灸期門隨年壯乳根主胷下滿痛亶中百壯天井主胷痹心痛太泉主胷膺痛肺俞雲門中府隠白期門魂門大陵主胷中痛少衝主胷痛口熱胷中痛引腰背心下嘔逆面無滋潤灸幽門隨年壯宂在俠巨闕兩邊相去各半寸一云一寸經渠丘墟主胷背急胷中彭太谿治胷滿痛乳腫賁膺欬逆上氣喉中作聲銅肝俞治欬引胷痛見欬逆少衝見傷寒中府治胷痛見肺急乳根治胷滿痛華盖治胷脇支滿痛引胷中欬逆上氣喘不能言明下同紫宫治胷脇支滿胷膺骨疼飲食不下嘔逆上氣煩心玉堂治胷滿不得喘息胷膺骨疼嘔吐寒痰上氣煩心幽門治胷中引痛心下煩悶逆氣裏急支滿不嗜食數欬健忘豐隆治厥逆胷痛如刺腹切痛明云氣刺不可忍見四支厥太淵治胷痹逆氣寒厥善噦嘔飲水欬嗽煩怒不得卧胷痹引背時寒閒使主之千閒使主胷痹背相引臨注主胷痹不得息魚際主痹走胷不得息浮白療胷滿胷痛明見欬逆

本神、颅息主治胸胁疼痛相互牵引，导致身体不能侧倾。《千金方》记载，太白主治胸胁胀满及剧烈疼痛。阳辅主治胸胁疼痛。环跳、至阴主治位置不固定的胸胁疼痛，以及腰胁相互牵引的急迫性疼痛。大包主治胸胁中部疼痛。丰隆的相关主治另见腹痛篇。丘墟主治如针刺般的胸痛、胸胁胀满及心痛。心痛时，可灸期门，灸壮数依年龄而定。乳根主治胸下胀满疼痛。膻中可灸一百壮。天井主治胸痹、心痛。太泉主治胸膺疼痛。肺俞、云门、中府、隐白、期门、魂门、大陵等穴主治胸中疼痛。少冲主治胸痛及口中发热。胸中疼痛牵引至腰背，心下呕逆，面色缺乏光泽，可灸幽门穴，灸壮数依年龄而定。该穴位于巨阙两侧，距巨阙各半寸；另有一说为一寸。经渠、丘墟主治胸背拘急、胸中胀满。太溪治疗胸部胀满疼痛、乳房肿胀、胸膺隆起、咳逆上气及喉中发出声响。《铜人》记载，肝俞治疗咳嗽牵引胸部疼痛，相关内容参见咳逆篇。少冲的相关内容参见伤寒篇。中府治疗胸痛，相关内容参见肺急篇。乳根治疗胸满及疼痛。华盖治疗胸胁支撑胀满、疼痛牵引胸中、咳逆上气、喘息及不能说话，《明堂》下卷记载相同。紫宫治疗胸胁支撑胀满、胸膺骨疼痛、饮食不能下咽、呕逆上气及心中烦闷。玉堂治疗胸满、呼吸不畅、胸膺骨疼痛、呕吐、寒痰上逆及心中烦闷。幽门治疗胸中牵引性疼痛、心下烦闷、逆气、腹内拘急胀满、食欲不振、频繁咳嗽及健忘。丰隆治疗厥逆、如针刺般的胸痛及腹部剧烈疼痛。《明堂》称这种疼痛如气刺，令人难以忍受，相关内容参见四肢厥冷篇。太渊治疗胸痹、气逆、寒厥、容易呃逆、呕吐、饮水、咳嗽、烦躁及不能平卧。胸痹疼痛牵引至背部、时有寒冷感，可取间使。《千金方》亦记载间使主治胸痹及背部相互牵引疼痛。临泣主治胸痹及呼吸不畅。鱼际主治痹痛走窜胸部、呼吸不畅。浮白治疗胸满、胸痛，《明堂》相关内容参见咳逆篇。

Benshen and Luxi treat pain involving both the chest and hypochondria, with pain pulling between the regions and preventing lateral bending. The Qian Jin Fang lists Taibai for distension and severe pain in the chest and hypochondria. Yangfu treats chest and hypochondriac pain. Huantiao and Zhiyin treat pain in the chest and hypochondria that shifts from place to place, as well as acute pain drawing between the waist and hypochondria. Dabao treats pain in the middle of the chest and hypochondria. Fenglong is also discussed under abdominal pain. Qiuxu treats stabbing chest pain, fullness of the chest and hypochondria, and heart pain. For heart pain, the text recommends moxibustion at Qimen, with the number of cones corresponding to the patient's age. Rugen treats fullness and pain below the chest. Danzhong is treated with one hundred moxa cones. Tianjing treats chest impediment and heart pain. Taiquan treats pectoral pain. Feishu, Yunmen, Zhongfu, Yinbai, Qimen, Hunmen, and Daling treat pain within the chest. Shaochong treats chest pain and heat in the mouth. For chest pain radiating toward the lower back, accompanied by nausea and retching below the heart and a dull facial appearance, the text recommends moxibustion at Youmen, with the number of cones corresponding to the patient's age. The point is located half a cun on either side of Juque; another account gives one cun. Jingqu and Qiuxu treat tightness of the chest and back and distension within the chest. Taixi treats painful fullness of the chest, breast swelling, prominence of the pectoral region, coughing with rebellious upward qi, and sounds in the throat. The Tong Ren states that Ganshu treats coughing with pain radiating into the chest; see the section on rebellious coughing. Shaochong is discussed under cold damage. Zhongfu treats chest pain; see the section on acute lung disorders. Rugen treats chest fullness and pain. Huagai treats distending fullness of the chest and hypochondria, pain radiating into the chest, coughing with rebellious upward qi, dyspnea, and inability to speak; the lower Ming Tang agrees. Zigong treats distending fullness of the chest and hypochondria, pain in the anterior chest bones, difficulty swallowing, vomiting, upward rebellious qi, and restlessness of the heart. Yutang treats chest fullness, difficulty breathing, pain in the anterior chest bones, vomiting, upward movement of cold phlegm, and restlessness of the heart. Youmen treats pain pulling through the chest, oppression below the heart, rebellious qi, internal abdominal tension and fullness, poor appetite, frequent coughing, and forgetfulness. Fenglong treats counterflow and reversal, stabbing chest pain, and severe abdominal pain. The Ming Tang describes the pain as resembling an unbearable stabbing sensation of qi; see the section on reversal coldness of the four limbs. Taiyuan treats chest impediment, rebellious qi, cold reversal, frequent hiccups, vomiting, drinking, coughing, irritability, and inability to lie down. For chest impediment radiating toward the back and accompanied by intermittent sensations of cold, Jianshi is indicated. The Qian Jin Fang also lists Jianshi for chest impediment with pain radiating toward the back. Linqi treats chest impediment with difficulty breathing. Yuji treats impediment pain spreading through the chest and difficulty breathing. Fubai treats chest fullness and pain; see rebellious coughing in the Ming Tang.

💡 穴位代码：本神 GB13；颅息 TE19；太白 SP3；阳辅 GB38；环跳 GB30；至阴 BL67；大包 SP21；丰隆 ST40；丘墟 GB40；期门 LR14；乳根 ST18；膻中 CV17；天井 TE10；肺俞 BL13；云门 LU2；中府 LU1；隐白 SP1；魂门 BL47；大陵 PC7；少冲 HT9；幽门 KI21；巨阙 CV14；经渠 LU8；太溪 KI3；肝俞 BL18；华盖 CV20；紫宫 CV19；玉堂 CV18；太渊 LU9；间使 PC5；临泣 GB41；鱼际 LU10；浮白 GB10。『太泉』在古籍传抄中可能涉及太渊等穴名异文，应以原始版本和穴位位置考订，不宜把两者当作确定相同。『心下』是上腹部、心窝附近的传统定位术语，不是指心脏本身。『口热』是口中发热的症状描述。『面无滋润』是面色少华、失去润泽的古代观察语言。古籍中的胸痹与现代缺血性心脏病症状有部分重叠，但胸痹不是冠心病的同义词。胸痛伴背部放射、呼吸困难、呕吐或冷汗，尤其须排除心肌缺血、主动脉疾病及肺栓塞。灸期门随年壮、幽门随年壮及膻中百壮均是历史记载，不能直接转化为现代施灸处方。

俞府療胷中痛下胷痹心痛不得息痛無常處臨泣主之胷痹灸胷堂見上氣㢘泉見上氣中府見欬主胷痛膽俞治胷脇不能轉銅明云胷脇滿見乾嘔丘墟治胷脇滿痛不得息明下云胷滿見太息下㢘治胷脇小腹痛大包治胷脇痛見上氣腎俞主兩脇引痛千肝俞脾俞志室主兩脇急痛支溝主脇腋急痛中管承滿主脇下堅痛腕骨陽谷主脇痛不得息膽俞章門主脇痛不得卧竅隂主脇痛欬逆尺澤少澤主短氣脇痛心煩闗元期門少商主脇下脹極泉治脇下滿痛銅鬲俞見嘔吐中膂俞見消渴竅隂見欬逆陽谷顱1712治脇痛見寒熱肝俞治兩脇急痛見欬逆腕骨治脇下痛不得息

俞府穴治疗胸中疼痛（《明堂下经》）。胸痹、心痛、呼吸不畅、疼痛位置不固定，临泣主治。胸痹可以灸胸堂穴，相关内容参见上气篇。廉泉穴相关内容参见上气篇。中府穴参见咳嗽篇，主治胸痛。胆俞穴治疗胸胁不能转动，《铜人》及《明堂》记载胸胁胀满，相关内容参见干呕篇。丘墟穴治疗胸胁胀满疼痛、呼吸不畅，《明堂》下卷又记载胸满及叹息。下廉穴治疗胸胁及小腹疼痛。大包穴治疗胸胁疼痛，相关内容参见上气篇。肾俞主治两胁牵引性疼痛。《千金方》记载，肝俞、脾俞、志室主治两胁急迫性疼痛。支沟主治胁部及腋下拘急疼痛。中脘、承满主治胁下坚硬疼痛。腕骨、阳谷主治胁痛及呼吸不畅。胆俞、章门主治胁痛、不能平卧。窍阴主治胁痛及咳嗽气逆。尺泽、少泽主治气短、胁痛及心中烦闷。关元、期门、少商主治胁下胀满。极泉治疗胁下胀满疼痛。《铜人》相关内容参见膈俞及呕吐篇。中膂俞参见消渴篇。窍阴参见咳逆篇。阳谷、颅息治疗胁痛，相关内容参见寒热篇。肝俞治疗两胁急迫性疼痛，相关内容参见咳逆篇。腕骨治疗胁下疼痛及呼吸不畅。

Shufu treats pain within the chest (lower Mingtang). For chest impediment, heart pain, inability to breathe comfortably, and pain with no fixed location, Linqi is indicated. For chest impediment, the text recommends moxibustion at the point called Xiongtang; see the section on upward qi. Lianquan is discussed under upward qi. Zhongfu is listed under coughing as a point for chest pain. Danshu treats inability to turn the chest and hypochondria. The Tong Ren and Ming Tang describe fullness of the chest and hypochondria; see the section on dry retching. Qiuxu treats painful fullness of the chest and hypochondria with difficulty breathing. The lower Ming Tang also mentions chest fullness and sighing. Xialian treats pain in the chest, hypochondria, and lower abdomen. Dabao treats pain in the chest and hypochondria; see upward qi. Shenshu is indicated for pain pulling along both hypochondria. The Qian Jin Fang lists Ganshu, Pishu, and Zhishi for acute pain in both hypochondria. Zhigou treats tight and painful sensations in the hypochondria and axilla. Zhongwan and Chengman treat hard pain below the ribs. Wangu and Yanggu treat hypochondriac pain with difficulty breathing. Danshu and Zhangmen treat hypochondriac pain with inability to lie down. Qiaoyin treats hypochondriac pain and rebellious coughing. Chize and Shaoze treat shortness of breath, hypochondriac pain, and restlessness of the heart. Guanyuan, Qimen, and Shaoshang treat distension below the ribs. Jiquan treats painful fullness below the ribs. The Tong Ren discusses Geshu under vomiting. Zhonglushu is discussed under wasting-thirst disorders. Qiaoyin is discussed under rebellious coughing. Yanggu and Luxi treat hypochondriac pain; see the section on alternating cold and heat. Ganshu treats acute pain in both hypochondria; see rebellious coughing. Wangu treats pain below the ribs with difficulty breathing.

💡 穴位代码：俞府 KI27；临泣 GB41；胸堂为古籍穴名，定位与现代标准穴位不能仅凭此字确定；廉泉 CV23；中府 LU1；胆俞 BL19；丘墟 GB40；下廉（下巨虚 ST39）；大包 SP21；肾俞 BL23；肝俞 BL18；脾俞 BL20；志室 BL52；支沟 TE6；中脘 CV12；承满 ST20；腕骨 SI4；阳谷 SI5；窍阴 GB44（须依语境区分足窍阴与手窍阴）；尺泽 LU5；少泽 SI1；关元 CV4；期门 LR14；少商 LU11；极泉 HT1；膈俞 BL17；中膂俞 BL29；颅息 TE19。『胸堂』不是可不加辨别地套用的标准 WHO 穴名；『颅1712』即颅囟，是颅息 TE19 的别名。『胁下坚痛』可能涉及肋骨、肋间肌、肝胆、胃肠及胸膜等部位，现代应按位置和伴随症状鉴别。胁痛牵涉呼吸、不能平卧、气短等表现时，需排除心肺和腹部急症。

腎俞治脇滿引小腹痛見勞瘵上㢘治飱泄腹脇痛滿狂走俠臍腹痛食不化喘息不能行太谿治腹脇痛連脊手足厥冷雲門療胷脇徹背痛華盖療胷脇痛見胷滿三里主脇鬲痛

肾俞穴治疗胁部胀满并牵引至小腹的疼痛，相关内容参见劳瘵篇。上廉穴治疗飧泄、腹部与胁部疼痛胀满、躁动奔走、脐旁腹痛、食物不能消化、喘息及不能行走。太溪穴治疗腹部及胁部疼痛，疼痛连及脊背，并伴有手足厥冷。云门穴治疗胸胁疼痛贯穿至背部。华盖穴治疗胸胁疼痛，相关内容参见胸满篇。足三里主治胁部及膈部疼痛。

Shenshu treats fullness of the hypochondria with pain radiating toward the lower abdomen; see the section on consumptive disease. Shanglian treats undigested-food diarrhea, painful fullness of the abdomen and hypochondria, agitation with restless running, abdominal pain beside the umbilicus, indigestion, panting, and inability to walk. Taixi treats abdominal and hypochondriac pain extending to the spine, accompanied by cold reversal of the hands and feet. Yunmen treats pain extending from the chest and hypochondria through to the back. Huagai treats pain in the chest and hypochondria; see the section on chest fullness. Zusanli treats pain involving the hypochondrium and diaphragm.

💡 穴位代码：肾俞 BL23；上廉（上巨虚 ST37，《铜人》载其治飧泄、腹胁支满、狂走、夹脐腹痛）；太溪 KI3；云门 LU2；华盖 CV20；足三里 ST36。『飧泄』指食物未充分消化而下泄的古代病候，不能直接等同于某一种现代吸收不良疾病。『狂走』描述躁动、奔走等行为表现，可能涉及多种神经精神或代谢因素。手足厥冷与腹痛、脊背痛同时出现，现代应排除循环障碍及急腹症。

鬲痛五噎氣哽

膈部疼痛、五噎及气哽。

Pain of the diaphragm, the five forms of esophageal obstruction, and obstruction of qi in the throat or esophagus.

💡 『鬲』通『膈』，指横膈及其附近区域。『五噎』是古代对吞咽阻塞、噎塞类病候的分类；『气哽』指气机阻塞、咽喉或胸膈有梗阻感。现代应与吞咽障碍、食管狭窄、食管动力障碍、胃食管反流及咽喉疾病比较。

承滿乳根療鬲氣明見噎亶中療胷鬲悶欬1033氣短喉鳴下鬲俞療鬲痛見痃癖商陽療胷鬲氣滿見喘足臨泣療胷鬲滿悶下見頬痛胷中鬲氣聚痛好吐灸厥隂俞隨年壯十隱白見嘔吐巨闕主鬲中不利明下同食竇銅同主鬲中雷鳴察察隱隱常有水聲明下云療鬲間鳴滀陸常有水聲胷鬲中氣灸闕俞隨年壯天池治胷鬲煩滿銅見胷滿亶中治鬲氣嘔吐涎沫率谷見痰鬲俞治鬲胃寒痰見嘔吐扶突等主氣哽千見喉鳴天突療氣鯁鯁明下見頭項腫章門療噎見積氣中庭治胷脇支滿噎塞銅大鍾治食噎不下見淋闗衝見霍亂天突治胷中氣噎見欬逆上氣亶中治胷中如塞明下岐伯云積氣成乾噎天突見嗽闗衝見吐㵼治氣噎明乳根療鬲氣不下食噎病譩譆療乆瘧背氣滿悶胷中氣噎下神堂見脊中府見肺氣療善噎下

承满、乳根治疗膈气，《明堂》相关内容参见噎塞篇。膻中治疗胸膈闷塞、咳嗽、气短及喉中鸣响，原文“欬1033”，1033为底本缺字。膈俞治疗膈部疼痛，相关内容参见痃癖篇。商阳治疗胸膈气满，相关内容参见喘证篇。足临泣治疗胸膈满闷，相关内容参见颊痛篇。胸中膈气聚结而疼痛、容易呕吐，可以灸厥阴俞，灸壮数依年龄而定。隐白穴相关内容参见呕吐篇。巨阙主治膈中气机不畅，《明堂》下卷记载相同。食窦穴，《铜人》亦记载其主治膈中雷鸣，声音细微而持续，常有水声。《明堂》下卷称其治疗膈间鸣响及水声持续不断。胸膈中气机壅塞，可以灸阙俞，灸壮数依年龄而定。天池穴治疗胸膈烦闷胀满，《铜人》相关内容参见胸满篇。膻中治疗膈气、呕吐及吐出涎沫。率谷相关内容参见痰饮篇。膈俞治疗膈胃寒痰，相关内容参见呕吐篇。扶突等穴主治气哽，《千金方》相关内容参见喉鸣篇。天突治疗气哽、吞咽梗阻，《明堂》下卷相关内容参见头项肿胀篇。章门治疗噎塞，相关内容参见积气篇。中庭治疗胸胁支撑胀满及噎塞。《铜人》记载，大钟治疗食物噎塞而不能下咽，相关内容参见淋证篇。关冲相关内容参见霍乱篇。天突治疗胸中气噎，相关内容参见咳逆上气篇。膻中治疗胸中如有东西堵塞。《明堂》下卷引岐伯之言，称积气形成干噎。天突相关内容参见咳嗽篇；关冲相关内容参见吐泻篇，亦治疗气噎。《明堂》记载，乳根治疗膈气不降、食物不能下咽及噎病。譩譆治疗久疟、背部气机满闷及胸中气噎。神堂相关内容参见脊中篇。中府相关内容参见肺气篇，治疗容易噎塞（《明堂下经》）。

Chengman and Rugen treat disorders described as qi in the diaphragm; the Ming Tang discusses them under esophageal obstruction. Danzhong treats oppression of the chest and diaphragm, coughing, shortness of breath, and wheezing in the throat. Geshu treats pain of the diaphragm; see the section on abdominal masses and accumulations. Shangyang treats fullness of qi in the chest and diaphragm; see the section on dyspnea. Foot Linqi treats oppressive fullness of the chest and diaphragm; see the section on cheek pain. When qi accumulates and causes pain in the chest and diaphragm, with a tendency to vomit, the text recommends moxibustion at Jueyinshu, using a number of cones corresponding to the patient's age. Yinbai is discussed under vomiting. Juque treats impaired movement of qi within the diaphragm; the lower Ming Tang agrees. Shidou, according to the Tong Ren, treats rumbling in the diaphragm, with faint, persistent sounds resembling water. The lower Ming Tang describes persistent sounds and water-like noises between the diaphragm. For qi obstruction in the chest and diaphragm, the text recommends moxibustion at Queyu, with the number of cones corresponding to the patient's age. Tianchi treats oppressive fullness of the chest and diaphragm; see the chest fullness section in the Tong Ren. Danzhong treats qi obstruction in the diaphragm, vomiting, and spitting of saliva or phlegm. Shuaigu is discussed under phlegm disorders. Geshu treats cold phlegm involving the diaphragm and stomach; see the section on vomiting. Futu and related points are listed for obstruction of qi; the Qian Jin Fang discusses this under throat wheezing. Tiantu treats obstruction of qi and difficulty swallowing; the lower Ming Tang discusses it under swelling of the head and neck. Zhangmen treats swallowing obstruction; see the section on accumulated qi. Zhongting treats distending fullness of the chest and hypochondria with obstruction of swallowing. The Tong Ren states that Dazhong treats food becoming stuck and failing to pass downward; see the urinary strangury section. Guanchong is discussed under cholera-like disorders. Tiantu treats obstruction of qi in the chest; see coughing with rebellious upward qi. Danzhong treats a sensation of obstruction in the chest. The lower Ming Tang quotes Qibo as saying that accumulated qi produces dry obstruction of swallowing. Tiantu is discussed under coughing. Guanchong is discussed under vomiting and diarrhea and is also indicated for obstruction of qi. The Ming Tang states that Rugen treats qi obstruction in the diaphragm, inability to swallow food, and esophageal obstruction. Yixi treats chronic malaria, oppressive fullness of qi in the back, and obstruction of qi in the chest. Shentang is discussed under spinal disorders. Zhongfu is discussed under lung qi disorders and treats a tendency to choke (lower Mingtang).

💡 穴位代码：承满 ST20；乳根 ST18；膻中 CV17；膈俞 BL17；商阳 LI1；足临泣 GB41；厥阴俞 BL14；隐白 SP1；巨阙 CV14；食窦 SP17；天池 PC1；率谷 GB8；扶突 LI18；天突 CV22；章门 LR13；中庭 CV16；大钟 KI4；关冲 TE1；譩譆 BL45；神堂 BL44。『阙俞』和『府俞』均须依版本、穴名系统进一步考订，不能只按字面指定 WHO 代码。『五噎』为历代医籍对噎塞病候的不同分类，并非固定不变的五种现代食管疾病。『干噎』指有噎塞感而食物或痰涎不易排出的情况。古代所说的膈气、气哽、胸中如塞，现代可能涉及食管、咽喉、胃、横膈、心肺等多种病变。进行性吞咽困难、吞咽疼痛、食物嵌塞、体重下降、呕血或黑便须及时就医。针灸不能替代对食管肿瘤、狭窄、异物及严重吞咽障碍的检查。

背痛胷背背脊餘見肩背勞瘵

背部疼痛、胸背疼痛及脊背疼痛，其余相关内容参见肩背及劳瘵篇。

Pain in the back, chest and back, and spinal region. For further information, see the sections on shoulder and back disorders and consumptive disease.

💡 古代『背痛』涵盖颈胸椎、肩胛区、背肌及脊柱周围疼痛。现代须区分肌肉筋膜疼痛、脊柱关节病、神经根病、骨折及内脏牵涉痛。

經渠丘墟主胷背急千附分主背痛引頭明下引頷鬲闗秩邊京骨主背惡寒痛脊强難俛仰崑崙主脊强背尻骨重鬲俞治背痛惡寒脊强俛仰難食飲不下嘔噦多涎唾胷噎悶銅意舍治明下有胷脇脹漓背痛惡風寒食不下嘔吐巨骨治背髆痛胷中有瘀血肩背不得屈伸而痛魄户治背3183痛見上氣神堂治背脊强急見肩痛氣户治胷背急見胷脇滿大椎治氣疰背髆拘急承筋治腰背拘急不容治胷背相引痛見痃癖經渠治胷背拘急胷滿膨膨見瘧魚際治痹走胷背痛見寒熱魄戸療背胛悶明見勞下同下云療肩膊間急痛背氣不能引顧欬逆上喘胃俞療背中氣上下行脊痛腹鳴下志室療背痛俛仰不得背痛灸巨闕等或灸胷堂千並上氣肺俞治背僂如1841背生時被客風拍著脊骨達於髓所致灸肺俞心俞鬲俞各三壯譩譆療温瘧寒瘧病瘧背悶氣滿腹脹氣3392明胷中痛引腰背千見胷脇列缺主胷背寒慄見肩痹魚際治痹走胷背痛銅見寒熱雲門療胷脇徹背痛明見上氣

经渠、丘墟主治胸背拘急。《千金方》记载，附分主治背痛牵引至头部；《明堂》下卷又记载疼痛牵引至下颌。膈关、秩边、京骨主治背部恶寒疼痛、脊柱强直及难以俯仰。昆仑主治脊柱强直、背部及尾骶骨沉重。膈俞治疗背痛、恶寒、脊柱强直、俯仰困难、饮食不能下咽、呕吐、干呕、涎液过多及胸部噎塞闷堵。《铜人》记载，意舍穴治疗相关症状；《明堂》下卷另有胸胁胀满、背痛、怕风怕冷、食物不能下咽及呕吐等内容。巨骨穴治疗背部肩胛疼痛，以及胸中有瘀血、肩背不能屈伸而疼痛。魄户穴治疗背痛，原文数字3183疑为残片，相关内容参见上气篇。神堂穴治疗背脊拘急强直，相关内容参见肩痛篇。气户穴治疗胸背拘急，相关内容参见胸胁满篇。大椎穴治疗气疰所致的背部肩胛拘急。承筋穴治疗腰背拘急。不容穴治疗胸背相互牵引疼痛，相关内容参见痃癖篇。经渠治疗胸背拘急、胸满及膨胀，相关内容参见疟疾篇。鱼际治疗痹痛走窜胸背，相关内容参见寒热篇。魄户治疗肩胛背部闷塞，《明堂》相关内容参见劳瘵篇；下卷又称其治疗肩胛之间拘急疼痛、背部气机不畅而不能回头、咳逆及喘息上气。胃俞治疗背部气机上下运行不畅、脊柱疼痛及腹鸣。志室治疗背痛、不能俯仰。背痛可以灸巨阙等穴，也可以灸胸堂，《千金方》亦有相关记载，内容参见上气篇。肺俞治疗背偻如龟（驼背，原文“1841”为底本缺字“龟”）。背部弯曲，古籍解释为生时受到外来风邪拍击脊骨，邪气深入骨髓所致，可以灸肺俞、心俞、膈俞，各三壮。譩譆治疗温疟、寒疟、疟疾、背部闷塞、气满及腹胀，以及眩晕（原文“3392”为底本缺字“眩”）。《明堂》记载胸中疼痛牵引腰背，《千金方》相关内容参见胸胁篇。列缺主治胸背寒冷颤栗，相关内容参见肩痹篇。鱼际治疗痹痛走窜胸背，《铜人》相关内容参见寒热篇。云门治疗胸胁疼痛贯穿至背部，《明堂》相关内容参见上气篇。

Jingqu and Qiuxu treat tightness of the chest and back. The Qian Jin Fang lists Fufen for back pain radiating toward the head; the lower Ming Tang also mentions radiation toward the jaw. Geguan, Zhibian, and Jinggu treat aversion to cold and pain in the back, spinal stiffness, and difficulty bending forward or backward. Kunlun treats spinal stiffness and a sensation of heaviness in the back and coccygeal region. Geshu treats back pain, aversion to cold, spinal stiffness, difficulty bending forward and backward, inability to swallow food, vomiting, retching, excessive saliva, and oppressive obstruction in the chest. The Tong Ren states that Yishe treats these conditions; the lower Ming Tang additionally lists fullness of the chest and hypochondria, back pain, sensitivity to wind and cold, difficulty swallowing, and vomiting. Jugu treats pain in the back of the shoulder and scapular region, as well as pain with inability to flex or extend the shoulder and back, attributed to blood stasis in the chest. Pohu treats back pain; See the section on upward qi. Shentang treats tightness and rigidity of the back and spine; see shoulder pain. Qihu treats tightness of the chest and back; see chest and hypochondriac fullness. Dazhui treats tightness of the scapular region attributed to qi disorders. Chengjin treats tightness of the lower back and back. Burong treats pain drawing between the chest and back; see the section on abdominal masses and accumulations. Jingqu treats tightness of the chest and back and distending fullness of the chest; see malaria. Yuji treats impediment pain spreading through the chest and back; see alternating cold and heat. Pohu treats oppression around the scapula; see consumptive disease in the Ming Tang. The lower volume further describes pain and tightness between the shoulder blades, inability to turn the head because of impaired movement of qi in the back, rebellious coughing, and upward panting. Weishu treats abnormal upward and downward movement of qi in the back, spinal pain, and intestinal rumbling. Zhishi treats back pain and inability to bend forward or backward. For back pain, the text recommends moxibustion at Juque and other points, or at Xiongtang. The Qian Jin Fang also mentions this under upward qi. Feishu treats a back hunched like a tortoise. The ancient explanation for a hunched back is that an external pathogenic wind struck the spine at birth and penetrated the marrow. The text recommends three moxa cones each at Feishu, Xinshu, and Geshu. Yixi treats warm malaria, cold malaria, recurrent malaria, oppression and fullness of qi in the back, and abdominal distension. It also treats dizziness. The Ming Tang describes pain within the chest radiating toward the lower back; the Qian Jin Fang discusses this under chest and hypochondriac disorders. Lieque treats coldness and shivering in the chest and back; see shoulder impediment. Yuji treats impediment pain spreading through the chest and back; see alternating cold and heat in the Tong Ren. Yunmen treats pain extending from the chest and hypochondria through to the back; see upward qi in the Ming Tang.

💡 穴位代码：经渠 LU8；丘墟 GB40；附分 BL41；膈关 BL46；秩边 BL54；京骨 BL64；昆仑 BL60；膈俞 BL17；意舍 BL49；巨骨 LI16；魄户 BL42；神堂 BL44；气户 ST13；大椎 GV14；承筋 BL56；不容 ST19；鱼际 LU10；胃俞 BL21；志室 BL52；巨阙 CV14；列缺 LU7；云门 LU2；肺俞 BL13；心俞 BL15；譩譆 BL45。『胸堂』及部分版本中的穴名须进一步考订，不宜自动等同于现代标准穴。『客风』、『邪入骨髓』是古代病因理论，不能用作现代脊柱病变的生物医学解释。『气疰』是古代病候名称。背痛、肩胛痛和脊柱拘急可与肌肉筋膜疼痛、关节功能障碍、神经根刺激等作临床对照，但古代穴位主治不能证明特定疾病由某条经络引起。背痛伴肢体无力、感觉丧失、大小便功能异常、发热、外伤或癌症病史，须及时医疗评估。

背疼乃作勞所致技藝之人與士女刻苦者多有此患士之書學女之鍼指皆刻苦而成背疼矣色勞者亦患之晉之景公是也惟膏肓為要穴予嘗於膏肓之側去脊骨四寸半隠隠微疼按之則疼甚謾以小艾灸三壯即不疼它日復連肩上疼却灸肩疼處愈方知千金方之阿是穴猶信云千每遇熱膏肓穴所在多出冷汗數年矣因灸而愈

背部疼痛往往是过度劳累造成的。从事技艺工作的人，以及刻苦勤奋的男女，多有这种毛病。读书的男子长期伏案学习，女子从事针线刺绣等精细工作，都可能因为过度劳累而引发背痛。房事劳倦者也可能患有此症，晋景公便是古代所举的例子。治疗此类背痛，膏肓穴是重要穴位。我曾在膏肓穴旁、距脊柱四寸半的地方，感觉到隐隐作痛，按压时疼痛更加明显。于是随意用小艾炷灸三壮，疼痛便消失了。另一天，疼痛又牵连至肩部，我便在肩痛的位置施灸，也痊愈了。由此我才知道，《千金方》所说的阿是穴确实可信。我每次遇热，膏肓穴所在部位常出冷汗，已有几年，后来因施灸而痊愈（原文此处作“千”，按文意当是“予”字之误）。

Back pain often results from excessive physical or mental exertion. People engaged in skilled occupations, as well as men and women who work diligently, frequently experience this complaint. Men who study and write for long periods, and women who undertake meticulous needlework and embroidery, may develop back pain through prolonged effort. Those who experience excessive sexual exertion may also suffer from it; Duke Jing of Jin is cited as an ancient example. The Gaohuang point is particularly important for this type of back pain. I once experienced a faint, dull pain beside Gaohuang, approximately four and a half cun from the spinal midline. The pain became more pronounced when pressed. I casually applied three small moxa cones, after which the pain disappeared. On another occasion, the pain again extended toward the shoulder. I applied moxibustion directly to the painful shoulder area, and the condition improved. Only then did I appreciate the value of the Ashi points described in the Qian Jin Fang. Whenever I became hot, the area of Gaohuang would often break into a cold sweat; this went on for several years and was relieved by moxibustion (the text writes qian here, evidently a miscopy of yu, "I").

💡 穴位：膏肓俞 BL43。『阿是穴』是以按压后出现明显疼痛或反应的位置作为治疗点的取穴概念，并非固定的经穴，也没有唯一 WHO 代码。古代『去脊骨四寸半』是对膏肓附近压痛位置的描述，不能据此直接推定为 BL43 的标准定位。王执中此处提供两个个人经验案例，具有针灸史和临床思维价值，但不等同于对照试验证据。『作劳』包括过度劳作、长期姿势固定和精神用力等传统理解，可与现代职业性肌肉骨骼疼痛、持续姿势负荷及肌筋膜疼痛作比较。古代将房劳纳入背痛病因，是其时代的病因理论，不能作为现代诊断依据。膏肓俞位于上背部，邻近胸膜和肺组织，深刺及不当施灸可能造成损伤。『冷汗』与发热时局部出汗的叙述，不足以确定自主神经或其他现代病理机制。

肩背酸痛肩髆肩臂餘見胷痛

肩背酸痛、肩胛及肩臂疼痛，其余相关内容参见胸痛篇。

Aching and pain in the shoulders and back, including the scapular region and shoulder and arm. For further information, see the section on chest pain.

💡 『肩髆』指肩胛及肩部附近。肩背痛可能涉及肩袖、肩峰下结构、肩胛周围肌群、颈椎神经根及内脏牵涉痛。

浮白治肩背不舉銅神堂療肩背連胷痛不可俛仰明下商陽治肩背急引缺盆痛見頷腫譩譆治温瘧肩背痛見鼻衂中府治肩背痛見肺氣附分治肩背急見腠理神堂治肩痛胷腹滿灑淅寒熱脊背急明同三焦俞治肩背急腰脊强不得俛仰涌泉主肩背頸項痛千天牖缺盆神道大杼天突水道巨骨主肩背痛鬲俞譩譆京門尺澤主肩背寒痓肩甲内㢘痛天柱治肩背痛欲折銅肝俞見腰痛療肩疼明曲

浮白穴治疗肩背不能抬举。《铜人》记载，神堂穴治疗肩背疼痛并牵连胸部，导致身体不能俯仰。《明堂》下卷记载，商阳穴治疗肩背拘急、疼痛牵引至缺盆，相关内容参见颔部肿胀篇。譩譆穴治疗温疟伴肩背疼痛，相关内容参见鼻衄篇。中府穴治疗肩背疼痛，相关内容参见肺气篇。附分穴治疗肩背拘急，相关内容参见腠理篇。神堂穴治疗肩痛、胸腹胀满、恶寒及寒热交替、脊背拘急，《明堂》记载相同。三焦俞穴治疗肩背拘急、腰脊强直及不能俯仰。涌泉穴主治肩背、颈项疼痛，《千金方》记载相同。天牖、缺盆、神道、大杼、天突、水道、巨骨等穴主治肩背疼痛。《千金方》另记载，膈俞、譩譆、京门、尺泽主治肩背恶寒、痉挛及肩胛骨内侧缘疼痛。天柱穴治疗肩背疼痛，如同将要折断一般，《铜人》记载相同。肝俞穴相关内容参见腰痛篇，亦治疗肩部疼痛。《明堂》记载曲……原文在『曲』字处截断。

Fubai treats inability to raise the shoulder and back. The Tong Ren states that Shentang treats pain extending from the shoulder and back into the chest, making bending forward and backward difficult. The lower Ming Tang states that Shangyang treats tightness of the shoulder and back, with pain radiating toward Quepen; see the section on swelling of the jaw. Yixi treats shoulder and back pain associated with warm malaria; see the section on nosebleed. Zhongfu treats shoulder and back pain; see lung qi disorders. Fufen treats tightness of the shoulder and back; see the section on the interstices of the skin. Shentang treats shoulder pain, fullness of the chest and abdomen, aversion to cold, alternating cold and heat, and tightness of the spine and back. The Ming Tang gives the same indications. Sanjiaoshu treats tightness of the shoulder and back, rigidity of the lumbar spine, and difficulty bending forward and backward. Yongquan treats pain in the shoulder, back, neck, and nape; the Qian Jin Fang agrees. Tianyou, Quepen, Shendao, Dazhu, Tiantu, Shuidao, and Jugu are listed for shoulder and back pain. The Qian Jin Fang additionally lists Geshu, Yixi, Jingmen, and Chize for aversion to cold in the shoulder and back, spasmodic stiffness, and pain along the inner border of the scapula. Tianzhu treats severe shoulder and back pain, as though the region were about to break; the Tong Ren agrees. Ganshu is discussed under lower back pain and also treats shoulder pain. The Ming Tang begins a further entry with the character 'Qu', but the supplied original text ends at this point.

💡 穴位代码：浮白 GB10；神堂 BL44；商阳 LI1；缺盆 ST12；譩譆 BL45；中府 LU1；附分 BL41；三焦俞 BL22；涌泉 KI1；天牖 TE16；神道 GV11；大杼 BL11；天突 CV22；水道 ST28；巨骨 LI16；膈俞 BL17；京门 GB25；尺泽 LU5；天柱 BL10；肝俞 BL18。『肩髆』、『肩甲内廉』是肩胛骨及其内侧缘的古代部位描述。肩背不能举、疼痛牵引至颈项，可能与肩关节活动障碍、肩袖病变、颈椎神经根病或周围神经受累有关。『寒痓』指寒冷伴随筋脉拘急、痉挛的古代病候，不是现代单一诊断。『腠理』指皮肤、肌肉之间的纹理及卫气出入的传统概念。针灸临床应先进行肩关节活动度、肌力、神经学及颈椎相关检查。原文最后只提供『曲』字，不能擅自补成曲池、曲垣或其他穴名；本段也不能视为卷五全文结束后的完整句子。

垣治肩痛周痹明同氣注肩髆拘急疼悶銅肩外俞治肩痹熱痛而寒至肘明同下云療肩痛發寒熱引項强

（曲）垣主治肩痛、周痹，《明堂》同；又治气滞聚于肩胛、肩膊拘急疼闷，《铜人》亦载。肩外俞主治肩痹、热痛而恶寒，寒意延及肘部，《明堂》同；下文又说可治肩痛、发寒热、牵引项部强直。

💡 原文首字残缺，应为『曲垣』SI13。引书简称：明＝《明堂》，铜＝《铜人》，千＝《千金》，甲乙＝《针灸甲乙经》；这是《资生经》按症状汇编各书主治的体例。周痹出《灵枢·周痹》，指痛处游走、上下循脉的痹证。肩外俞 SI14。今日对应肩周炎、肩背肌筋膜疼痛综合征、颈源性肩痛；穴位主治仍用于临床和考试，但要先排除颈椎病、心绞痛放射痛等需要西医处理的原因。

肩井治頸項不得顧肩髆悶兩手不得向頭或因撲傷雲門見胷滿秉風治肩痛不能舉肩貞治肩中熱痛見風痹天宗治肩胛痛天1495明下同治肩痛引項不得顧前谷療3183胛小指痛養老療肩欲折下見臂青靈療

💡 肩井 GB21、云门 LU2、秉风 SI12、肩贞 SI9、天宗 SI11、天窗 SI16、前谷 SI2、养老 SI6、青灵 HT2。『天1495』即天窗 SI16（1495为底本缺字“窗”）；『3183胛』的3183为底本缺字，疑为“髆”。肩井是肩颈痛的常用穴，但针刺过深有气胸风险，孕妇肩井在传统上也属慎用。肩井属足少阳经与阳维脉交会穴，所以既治颈项不利，也治扑伤。今天这些穴位仍用于肩颈疼痛，原理多从局部肌肉放松、镇痛和神经调节解释。

肩不舉不能帶衣肩髎療肩重不舉見臂養老天柱主肩痛欲折天井主肩痛不可屈伸曲池天窌主肩重痛不舉巨骨主肩中痛不能動摇明下同肩外俞主肩甲痛而寒至肘後谿主肩臑痛淵腋主肩腋前痛與胷相引

（青灵治）肩不能抬举、不能自己穿衣。肩髎主治肩部沉重、不能抬举，见『臂』门。养老、天柱主治肩痛像要折断。天井主治肩痛、不能屈伸。曲池、天髎主治肩部沉重疼痛、不能抬举。巨骨主治肩中疼痛、不能活动摇动，《明堂》下同。肩外俞主治肩胛痛而发冷，寒意到肘。后溪主治肩臑疼痛。渊腋主治肩与腋前疼痛，与胸部相互牵引。

💡 肩髎 TE14、天柱 BL10、天井 TE10、曲池 LI11、天窌即天髎 TE15、巨骨 LI16、后溪 SI3、渊腋 GB22。『不能带衣』指穿衣时手臂上不去，是肩关节外展、后伸受限的古代描述，今天对应肩周炎或肩袖损伤。天柱属膀胱经，在项后，说明古人把肩痛与太阳经走向（项—肩背）连起来。渊腋在侧胸，位置接近腋前线第四肋间，针刺要斜刺或平刺，避免气胸。现代考试与临床会把这几条归入『肩痹、肩凝』的局部取穴。

天髎治肩肘痛引頸項急寒熱缺盆中痛汗不出胷中煩悶銅章門治厥逆肩臂不舉青靈治肩臂不舉不能帶衣肩髎治肩重不能舉臂肘巨骨治肩臂不得屈伸見背痛居髎治肩引胷臂急見手痛臑腧治寒熱肩腫引胛中痛臂酸無力支溝療肩臂酸重見腋

💡 天髎 TE15、章门 LR13、青灵 HT2、肩髎 TE14、巨骨 LI16、居髎 GB29、臑腧 SI10、支沟 TE6。缺盆指锁骨上窝。章门是脾之募穴、脏会穴，治厥逆肩臂不举的取意是疏肝健脾、通调气机，这是理论推演，现代证据很少，不宜当作肩痛首选。『厥逆』指四肢逆冷或气血逆乱，不是现代的『昏厥』。居髎在髋部，说它治肩引胸臂急，反映经络远端取穴的思路（足少阳经上下相应），今天考试仍会考经络循行，但临床很少这样用。

闗衝主肩臂酸重腕骨主肩臂疼天宗主肩重臂痛章門治厥逆肩臂不舉銅青靈治肩臂不舉不能帶衣肩髎治肩重不能舉臂肘巨骨治肩臂不得屈伸見背痛闗衝主肩臂酸痛支溝療肩臂酸重明下見腋

关冲主治肩臂酸重。腕骨主治肩臂疼痛。天宗主治肩沉重、臂疼痛。章门主治厥逆、肩臂不能抬举，《铜人》所载。青灵主治肩臂不能抬举、不能穿衣。肩髎主治肩重、手臂和肘不能抬举。巨骨主治肩臂不能屈伸，见『背痛』门。关冲主治肩臂酸痛。支沟主治肩臂酸重，《明堂》下见『腋』门。

Guanchong treats aching, heavy shoulder and arm. Wangu treats shoulder and arm pain. Tianzong treats heavy shoulder and painful arm. Jianliao treats heavy shoulder and inability to lift the arm and elbow. Jugu treats inability to flex or extend shoulder and arm, also under back pain. Guanchong treats aching, painful shoulder and arm. Zhigou treats aching, heavy shoulder and arm, as in the Mingtang, also under the axilla.

💡 关冲 TE1、腕骨 SI4、天宗 SI11、章门 LR13、青灵 HT2、肩髎 TE14、巨骨 LI16、支沟 TE6。这一段与上一段有明显重复（章门、青灵、肩髎、巨骨、关冲、支沟再次出现），这是《资生经》把不同来源的条文分别收录、未完全去重造成的，学习时应把重复看作『多部书都这样记载』。手少阳三焦经与手太阳小肠经的井穴、原穴和络穴常用于肩臂病，这与两经循行经过肩部相符。

列缺主肩背寒慄少氣不足以息寒厥交兩手而鶩凡實則肩背熱背汗出四支暴腫虛則肩寒慄氣不足以息天井療頸項及肩背痛見肘痛腰背痛灸三焦俞大椎治背3183急見勞下焦俞療背痛身熱肩背酸疼

列缺主治肩背发冷颤抖、气少而呼吸不足；寒厥时双手交叉、精神恍惚惊乱。凡是实证，就肩背发热、背部出汗、四肢突然肿胀；虚证则肩部发冷颤抖、气短不足以呼吸。天井主治颈项及肩背疼痛（见『肘痛』门）。腰背痛，灸三焦俞。大椎治背髆拘急（原文“背3183急”），见『虚劳』门。下焦俞主治背痛、身热、肩背酸痛。

Lieque treats chills and shivering in the shoulder and back, shortness of breath with insufficient breathing, and cold reversal in which the person crosses the hands and is startled. In excess patterns, the shoulder and back are hot, the back sweats, and the four limbs swell suddenly; in deficiency patterns, the shoulder shivers with cold and the qi is too short for breathing. Tianjing treats pain of the neck, shoulder and back (see elbow pain). For lumbar-back pain, moxibust Sanjiaoshu. Dazhui treats tension of the back and shoulder blades, see consumptive disorders. Xiajiaoshu treats back pain, fever, and aching shoulder and back.

💡 列缺 LU7、天井 TE10、三焦俞 BL22、大椎 GV14。『下焦俞』原文如此，所指不明（本书卷三亦有“下焦俞疗背痛身热”）。这一条取自《甲乙经》：『实则肩背热痛，汗出，四肢暴肿；虚则肩背寒栗，少气不足以息』，讲手太阴肺经的虚实：实证多热、虚证多寒。『寒厥』指阳气衰、四肢寒冷。今天可理解为肺气虚、呼吸功能弱时的畏寒、呼吸表浅，但不能把这些症状简单归到列缺，现代需做呼吸、心脏检查。大椎为督脉穴，也是手足三阳经与督脉的交会穴，常用于退热和颈肩背疾病。

諸家鍼灸之詳矣當隨病證鍼灸之或背上先疼遂率引肩上疼者乃是膏肓為患千金外臺固云按之自覺牽引於肩中是也當灸膏肓俞則肩背自不疼矣

各家针灸书对此已记载得很详细了，应当根据具体病证来针灸。如果先是背上疼痛，随即牵连到肩上疼痛，这是膏肓出了问题，《千金》《外台》本来就说，按压时自己觉得有牵引到肩中的感觉，就是这种情况。应当灸膏肓俞，那么肩背自然就不疼了。

Many authorities have recorded acupuncture and moxibustion for this in detail, and one should choose by the pattern. If pain first appears in the back and then pulls up into the shoulder, the cause is the Gaohuang region. The Qianjin and Waitai already say that pressing there produces a pulling sensation into the shoulder. One should moxa Gaohuangshu, and then the shoulder and back will no longer hurt.

💡 膏肓俞 BL43，在第四胸椎棘突下旁开3寸。『膏肓』在古代医学中指心下膈上的深部位置，是病邪深入、难治的地方（左传『病入膏肓』）。《千金》《外台》都载有灸膏肓的方法，《资生经》特别推崇。这是王执中『按压有反应处取穴』的经验，接近今日的阿是穴和压痛点取穴。对应现代：上背部肌筋膜疼痛、肩胛周围肌肉紧张，也可能是颈椎或胸椎关节的牵涉痛。灸时需注意避免烫伤，且若背痛伴胸闷、呼吸困难要先排除心肺疾病。

予嘗肩背痛已灸膏肓肩痛猶未巳遂灸肩井三壯而愈以此知雖灸膏肓而他處亦不可不灸云

我曾经肩背疼痛，已经灸了膏肓，肩痛还没好，于是又灸肩井三壮，才治好。由此可知，即使灸了膏肓，其他部位也不能不灸。

I once had shoulder and back pain; after moxa at Gaohuang the shoulder pain had not yet stopped, so I moxaed Jianjing for three cones and was cured. From this I learned that even after moxa at Gaohuang, one cannot leave other places unmoxaed.

💡 这是王执中本人的经验记录，是个人病案，不是对照试验，现在只能看作经验而不能当作疗效证据。肩井 GB21，三壮指艾炷灸三次。重点是『局部取穴加远端取穴』的配合：背部的膏肓配肩部的肩井，说明古人并不死守一穴。学习时可记：病在局部，用阿是穴或邻近穴；病在背，兼顾夹脊、背俞穴。

肩痹痛不仁不舉

（标题）肩痹：疼痛、麻木不仁、不能抬举。

(Heading) Shoulder impediment: pain, numbness, and inability to lift the arm.

💡 痹指气血闭阻不通的一类病，《素问·痹论》分风寒湿三气杂至。『不仁』指麻木、感觉迟钝；『不举』指抬不起来。今天对应肩周炎、颈椎病神经根压迫、中风后肩部问题等。

天井主肩痛痿痹不仁不可屈伸肩肉麻木曲垣主肩甲周痹肩貞肩髃闗衝主肩中熱頭不可顧曲池天窌主肩重痛不舉清冷泉明同陽谷主

天井主治肩痛、痿痹麻木不仁、不能屈伸，肩部肌肉麻木。曲垣主治肩胛周痹。肩贞、肩髃、关冲主治肩中发热、头不能回顾。曲池、天髎主治肩沉重疼痛、不能抬举；清冷泉，《明堂》同。阳谷主治（接下一行）

Tianjing treats shoulder pain, atrophic impediment with numbness, inability to flex or extend, and numbness of the shoulder muscles. Quyuan treats periarthralgia of the scapula. Jianzhen, Jianyu and Guanchong treat heat within the shoulder and inability to turn the head. Quchi and Tianliao treat heavy, painful shoulder that cannot be raised; Qinglengquan is the same in the Mingtang. Yanggu treats (continued on the next line).

💡 天井 TE10、曲垣 SI13、肩贞 SI9、肩髃 LI15、关冲 TE1、曲池 LI11、天髎 TE15、清冷泉 TE11、阳谷 SI5。『痿』指肢体萎软，『痹』指闭阻，二者在古代有时并称。肩髃是手阳明与阳跷脉的交会穴，是治肩臂病最常用的穴位之一，今天仍是肩周炎、上肢瘫痪的常用穴。

肩不舉不得帶衣天井療肘痛引肩不屈伸見肘肩外俞治肩痹曲垣治肩痛周痹並見肩背

（阳谷主治）肩不能抬举、不能穿衣。天井主治肘痛牵引到肩、不能屈伸，见『肘』门。肩外俞主治肩痹，曲垣主治肩痛周痹，都见『肩背』门。

(Yanggu treats) inability to lift the shoulder or put on clothes. Tianjing treats elbow pain pulling to the shoulder with inability to flex or extend, listed under the elbow. Jianwaishu treats shoulder impediment and Quyuan treats shoulder pain and periarthralgia; both are also listed under shoulder and back.

💡 阳谷 SI5、天井 TE10、肩外俞 SI14、曲垣 SI13。这一段是目录式的交叉引用：『见肘』『见肩背』表示在其他门类里有更详细的条文。学习要点：手太阳小肠经的阳谷、肩外俞、曲垣沿经脉走向上达肩胛，所以多用于肩胛区疼痛、肩不能抬举。

兩肩頭冷疼尤不可忽予屢見將中風人臂骨脱臼不與肩相連接多有治不愈者要之纔覺肩上冷疼必先灸肩髃等穴毋使至於此極可也予中年每遇寒月肩上多冷常以手掌心撫摩之夜卧則多以被擁之僅能不冷後灸肩髃方免此患盖肩髃係兩手之安否環跳係兩足之安否不可不灸也

两肩头发冷疼痛尤其不可忽视。我屡次见到将要中风的人，手臂骨脱臼、与肩不相连接，多数治不好。总之，刚觉得肩上冷痛时，一定要先灸肩髃等穴，不要让病发展到这种地步。我中年时每逢寒冷月份肩上常发冷，常用手掌抚摩，夜里睡觉多用被子裹护，勉强才不冷。后来灸肩髃，才免除了这个毛病。因为肩髃关系到两手的安与否，环跳关系到两脚的安与否，不可不灸。

Cold pain at the tips of both shoulders must not be ignored. I have often seen people about to suffer wind stroke whose arm bone dislocates and no longer connects to the shoulder; many of these could not be cured. In short, as soon as you notice cold pain in the shoulder, first moxa Jianyu and similar points so that it does not reach that extreme. In middle age, whenever it was cold, my shoulders felt cold; I rubbed them with my palm and covered them with the quilt at night to stay barely warm. Only after moxa at Jianyu was I spared this trouble. Jianyu governs whether the two hands are well or not, and Huantiao governs the two legs, so neither can go unmoxaed.

💡 肩髃 LI15、环跳 GB30。『中风』在古代指突然昏倒、半身不遂，近于现代脑卒中。『臂骨脱臼不与肩相连』通常是偏瘫后肩关节周围肌肉松弛造成的肩关节半脱位，现代康复医学确有此问题，而且肩痛是中风后常见并发症。作者主张预防性灸肩髃，今天可理解为早期保护肩关节、促进上肢功能恢复；但『肩冷痛就预示中风』不能当作诊断依据，若肩冷痛伴一侧肢体无力、言语不清，应立即急诊。灸法需防烫伤，中风后感觉减退的人尤其要小心。

臂痛臂無力

（标题）臂痛、臂无力。

(Heading) Arm pain and weakness of the arm.

💡 对应现代：颈椎神经根病、臂丛神经损伤、肌肉萎缩、肌无力、肩肘疾病等。古代条文多由症状直接配穴，今天需要先鉴别病因。

曲池療肘臂偏細明下見肘肩髃療臂細無力痠疼臂冷而緩明臂臑肩髃療臂細無力手不得向頭少海見瘰癧乳根聴宫療臂疼中渚孔最支正肘髎療肘臂酸痛見肘痛閒使療臂腫痛屈伸難下肩髎療肩重不舉臂痛扁骨即肩髃療肩中熱指臂痛乳根治臂腫銅太淵治臂内㢘痛居髎治腰引小腹痛肩引胷臂攣急手臂不得舉而至肩臂臑見瘰癧肘髎治臂痛不舉見肘聴宫治臂痛孔最治臂厥痛可鍼陽谷治臂腕外側痛不舉液門見咽腫前谷治臂

曲池主治肘臂一侧细瘦，《明堂》下见『肘』门。肩髃主治臂细无力、酸痛、臂冷而弛缓（《明堂》）。臂臑、肩髃主治臂细无力、手不能举到头；少海见『瘰疬』门。乳根、听宫主治臂痛。中渚、孔最、支正、肘髎主治肘臂酸痛，见『肘痛』门。间使主治臂肿痛、屈伸困难（《明堂下经》）。肩髎主治肩重不能举、臂痛。扁骨即肩髃，主治肩中热、手指及臂疼痛。乳根主治臂肿，《铜人》所载。太渊主治臂内廉疼痛。居髎主治腰牵引小腹痛、肩牵引胸臂拘急，手臂不能抬到肩；臂臑见『瘰疬』门。肘髎主治臂痛不能抬举，见『肘』门。听宫主治臂痛。孔最主治臂厥痛，可以针刺。阳谷主治臂腕外侧痛、不能抬举；液门见『咽肿』门。前谷主治臂（接下一行）

Quchi treats thinness on one side of the elbow and arm, see elbow. Jianyu treats thin, weak, sore arm, cold and slack, (Mingtang). Binao and Jianyu treat a thin weak arm and inability to raise the hand to the head. Shaohai is listed under scrofula. Rugen and Tinggong treat arm pain. Zhongzhu, Kongzui, Zhizheng and Zhouliao treat aching elbow and arm, see elbow pain. Jianshi treats swollen, painful arm with difficulty flexing and extending. Jianliao treats heavy shoulder that cannot lift and arm pain. Bian bone is Jianyu, treating heat in the shoulder and pain in fingers and arm. Rugen treats swollen arm (Tongren). Taiyuan treats pain on the medial arm. Juliao treats lumbar pain pulling on the lower abdomen and shoulder pulling on chest with arm spasm, arm unable to lift to the shoulder; Binao is listed under scrofula. Zhouliao treats arm pain with inability to lift, see elbow. Tinggong treats arm pain. Kongzui treats arm reversal pain, and may be needled. Yanggu treats pain on the lateral wrist and arm with inability to lift; Yemen is listed under sore throat swelling. Qiangu treats the arm (continued).

💡 曲池 LI11、肩髃 LI15、少海 HT3、乳根 ST18、听宫 SI19、中渚 TE3、孔最 LU6、支正 SI7、肘髎 LI12、间使 PC5、肩髎 TE14、扁骨即肩髃、太渊 LU9、居髎 GB29、臂臑 LI14、阳谷 SI5、液门 TE2、前谷 SI2。『臂偏细』指单侧上肢萎缩，近似现代的偏瘫后肌萎缩或神经损伤。乳根在胸部（乳下），古人说它治臂肿，可能与乳房、腋部淋巴回流有关；今日考试上它是胃经穴位，主治乳房疾病为主，治臂痛属少见的古代记载，不宜随便临床套用。孔最是肺经郄穴，『臂厥』指臂部气血逆乱疼痛。『可针』为特别说明此穴可以针。

不得舉陽池治因折傷手腕捉物不得肩臂痛不舉極泉治臂肘厥寒清淵治臑從肩臂不舉不得帶

（前谷主治臂）不能抬举。阳池主治因折伤手腕、握物不得、肩臂痛不能抬举。极泉主治臂肘厥冷发寒。（渊）主治臑部，从肩到臂不能抬举、不能穿（衣）。

(Qiangu treats the arm) with inability to lift. Yangchi treats injury of the wrist from fracture, inability to hold objects, and shoulder and arm pain with inability to lift. Jiquan treats cold reversal of arm and elbow. (Yuan) treats the upper arm, with inability to lift from shoulder to arm and inability to put on (clothes).

💡 阳池 TE4、极泉 HT1、渊（腋）GB22 或臂臑类，原文此处『渊』疑为渊腋或淸冷渊，须对照原书。『清冷渊』TE11 在肘上，原文『极泉治臂肘厥寒清渊治臑』应为『极泉治臂肘厥寒；清冷渊治臑……』的连写，故渊＝清冷渊 TE11，我保守标注为 TE11。阳池是三焦经原穴，治手腕痛是局部取穴，今天用于腕关节疼痛、腕管综合征、手指无力等。极泉在腋窝，针刺要避开腋动脉和臂丛神经。

衣養老治肩欲折臂如㧞臂明下作手痛不能自上下臑腧治臂酸無力見肩痛章門治厥逆肩臂不舉巨骨治肩臂不得屈伸而痛見肩痛臑㑹治臂痛不能舉氣腫痙痛肩髃治手臂攣急見偏風尺澤肩貞治風痹手臂不舉合谷治痿臂陽谿治臂不舉見肘天宗五里等治臂痛後谿治臂急竅隂等腕骨治臂不伸附分治臂不仁並肘巨骨前谷主臂不舉尺澤關衝外闗竅隂主臂不及頭前腋主臂攣急手不上舉神門少海主臂攣顏色焦枯勞氣失精肩臂痛不得上頭肩髃百壯掖門主臂痛肩窌天宗陽谷主臂痛前谷後谿陽谿

（渊治臑，从肩臂不举、不能穿）衣。养老主治肩痛如折、臂痛如被拔；《明堂》下作『手痛不能自行上下』。臑腧主治臂酸无力，见『肩痛』门。章门主治厥逆、肩臂不举。巨骨主治肩臂不能屈伸而痛，见『肩痛』门。臑会主治臂痛不能抬举、气肿、痉痛。肩髃主治手臂拘挛，见『偏风』门。尺泽、肩贞主治风痹、手臂不能抬举。合谷主治痿臂。阳溪主治臂不能抬举，见『肘』门。天宗、五里等主治臂痛。后溪主治臂拘急；窍阴等。腕骨主治臂不能伸。附分主治臂麻木不仁。并见『肘』门。巨骨、前谷主治臂不能抬举。尺泽、关冲、外关、窍阴主治臂不能举到头。前腋主治臂拘挛、手不能上举。神门、少海主治臂拘挛、面色焦枯、劳气失精、肩臂痛不能上举到头。肩髃（灸）一百壮。腋门主治臂痛；肩髎、天宗、阳谷主治臂痛；前谷、后溪、阳溪（接下一行）

(Yuan treats the upper arm; shoulder and arm cannot lift or put on) clothes. Yanglao treats shoulder pain as if breaking and arm pain as if pulled out; the Mingtang below says hand pain with inability to move up and down by oneself. Naoshu treats weak, sore arm, see shoulder pain. Zhangmen treats reversal counterflow with inability to lift shoulder and arm. Jugu treats inability to flex or extend shoulder and arm with pain, see shoulder pain. Naohui treats arm pain with inability to lift, qi swelling, and cramping pain. Jianyu treats spasm of the hand and arm, see hemiplegia. Chize and Jianzhen treat wind impediment with inability to lift the hand and arm. Hegu treats atrophy of the arm. Yangxi treats arm that cannot lift, see elbow. Tianzong, Wuli and others treat arm pain. Houxi treats tense arm; Qiaoyin and others. Wangu treats arm that cannot extend. Fufen treats numb arm. Jugu and Qiangu treat arm that cannot lift. Chize, Guanchong, Waiguan and Qiaoyin treat arm that cannot reach the head. Qianye treats arm spasm and hand that cannot lift. Shenmen and Shaohai treat arm spasm, scorched dry complexion, taxation qi and loss of essence, and shoulder and arm pain with inability to raise to the head. Moxa Jianyu one hundred cones. Yemen treats arm pain; Jianliao, Tianzong and Yanggu treat arm pain; Qiangu, Houxi, Yangxi (continued).

💡 养老 SI6、臑腧 SI10、章门 LR13、巨骨 LI16、臑会 TE13、肩髃 LI15、尺泽 LU5、肩贞 SI9、合谷 LI4、阳溪 LI5、天宗 SI11、五里 LI13（手五里）、后溪 SI3、窍阴（手足皆有，此处多指足窍阴 GB44）、腕骨 SI4、附分 BL41、前谷 SI2、关冲 TE1、外关 TE5、神门 HT7、少海 HT3、液门 TE2（原文『掖门』）、肩髎 TE14。『前腋』疑为『前谷』的误字，须对照原书。『劳气失精』指虚劳耗损气、精，属虚劳范畴。『肩髃百壮』是极大灸量，现代灸法不会用这样的壮数，因为瘢痕灸烫伤风险高。『气肿』指气滞所致的肿。这段汇集多部书的主治，只作文献参考，不能当作处方。

主臂重痛肘攣太泉經渠主臂内㢘痛腕骨曲池前谷陽谷主臂腕急腕外側痛脱如拔腕骨天宗主肩臂痛見肩背痛列缺主手臂身熱後谿三里曲池療臂痛明見肘痛

（阳溪）主治臂沉重疼痛、肘拘挛。太渊、经渠主治臂内侧疼痛。腕骨、曲池、前谷、阳谷主治臂腕拘急、腕外侧疼痛，像要脱落、被拔出一样。腕骨、天宗主治肩臂痛，见『肩背痛』门。列缺主治手臂、身体发热。后溪、（手）三里、曲池主治臂痛，《明堂》见『肘痛』门。

(Yangxi) treats heavy, painful arm and spasm of the elbow. Taiyuan and Jingqu treat pain on the inner arm. Wangu, Quchi, Qiangu and Yanggu treat tense wrist and arm and pain on the outer wrist as if being pulled out. Wangu and Tianzong treat shoulder and arm pain, see shoulder and back pain. Lieque treats heat in the hand, arm and body. Houxi, Sanli and Quchi treat arm pain, see elbow pain in the Mingtang.

💡 太渊 LU9（原文『太泉』是避唐讳字，渊→泉）、经渠 LU8、腕骨 SI4、曲池 LI11、前谷 SI2、阳谷 SI5、天宗 SI11、列缺 LU7、后溪 SI3、三里（手三里 LI10）。注意『太泉』即太渊，唐代避高祖李渊讳改字，古书常见，考试要认得。『脱如拔』形容疼痛剧烈，像手腕要掉下来。手阴经内侧痛取肺经太渊、经渠，是按经络循行取穴；手阳经外侧痛取小肠、大肠经穴。这是『循经取穴』的典型例子。

腋痛腋腫

（标题）腋痛、腋肿。

(Heading) Axillary pain and axillary swelling.

💡 腋下肿痛在古代包括腋窝淋巴结肿大、腋下脓肿、痈疽、乳腺疾病的淋巴转移等。现代遇到腋窝肿块，应先排查感染和肿瘤，不能只靠针灸。

足臨泣治胷滿缺盆中及腋下腫明下同馬刀瘍瘻善囓脣天牖中腫淫濼䯒痠目3392枕骨合顱痛灑淅振寒銅丘墟治腋下腫痿厥坐不能起髀嘔中痛目生翳膜

足临泣主治胸满、缺盆中及腋下肿胀，《明堂》下同；又治马刀疮、瘘、喜咬嘴唇；天牖中肿；淫泺、胫酸；目眩（原文“目3392”）、枕骨及合颅疼痛、怕冷颤抖，《铜人》所载。丘墟主治腋下肿、痿厥、坐位不能起立、髀部及（胸）中疼痛、目生翳膜。

Zulinqi treats chest fullness, swelling in the supraclavicular fossa and under the axilla (the Mingtang is the same below); also sword-shaped scrofulous sores and fistulae, frequent biting of the lips, swelling of Tianyou, aching shins, dizzy vision, pain of the occiput and skull sutures, and shivering with cold, per the Tongren. Qiuxu treats axillary swelling, atrophy reversal with inability to rise from sitting, pain of the thigh and chest, and nebula in the eyes.

💡 足临泣 GB41、天牖 TE16、丘墟 GB40。『马刀』『瘘』指颈腋部的淋巴结结核（瘰疬）：长形的叫马刀，圆的叫挟瘿。这是古代很重要的一类病，现代对应颈腋淋巴结结核、淋巴结炎或淋巴瘤。『痿厥』指下肢痿软无力并有逆冷。足少阳胆经经过颈、腋、胁，所以腋下肿多取胆经穴，是循经取穴的又一例子。『目3392』即目眩，3392为底本缺字。临床上腋下肿块切不可只灸针，必须排除肿瘤和感染。

腿䯒痠轉筋卒疝小腹堅寒熱頸腫譩譆治腋拘攣暴脈急引脇痛少府治股腋攣急見憂悲少海治肘腋腫小腹痛少海治肘攣腋脇下痛四支不舉支溝治肩臂痠重脇腋痛四支不舉天池見鬲痛膽俞委陽見尸厥陽輔見膝痛治腋下腫間使治掌中熱腋腫肘攣見狂地五㑹陽輔申脈委陽天池臨泣俠谿等見瘻主腋下腫太陵主肘攣腋腫臨泣主腋下腫胷中滿丘墟陽蹻主腋下腫寒熱頸腫少海療腋下瘰3371見瘰3371承筋等主掖腫見痔

（丘墟还主治）腿胫酸痛、转筋、突发疝气、小腹坚硬、寒热、颈肿。譩譆主治腋部拘挛、突然脉急牵引胁痛。少府主治大腿与腋部拘挛急，见『忧悲』门。少海主治肘腋肿、小腹痛；又主治肘拘挛、腋胁下痛、四肢不能举。支沟主治肩臂酸重、胁腋痛、四肢不能举。天池见『膈痛』门；胆俞、委阳见『尸厥』门；阳辅见『膝痛』门，治腋下肿。间使主治掌中热、腋肿、肘拘挛，见『狂』门。地五会、阳辅、申脉、委阳、天池、临泣、侠溪等见『瘘』门，主治腋下肿。太陵主治肘拘挛、腋肿。临泣主治腋下肿、胸中胀满。丘墟、阳跷主治腋下肿、寒热、颈肿。少海主治腋下瘰（疬）；见『瘰疬』门。承筋等主治腋肿，见『痔』门。

(Qiuxu also treats) aching thighs and shins, cramps, sudden hernia, hard lower abdomen, chills and fever, and neck swelling. Yixi treats axillary spasm and sudden tight pulse pulling on the flank. Shaofu treats spasm of the thigh and axilla, see grief and worry. Shaohai treats swollen elbow and axilla with lower abdominal pain, and also elbow spasm, pain under the axilla and flank, and inability to lift the four limbs. Zhigou treats aching, heavy shoulder and arm, pain in the flank and axilla, and inability to lift the limbs. Tianchi is listed under diaphragm pain; Danshu and Weiyang under corpse reversal; Yangfu under knee pain, treating axillary swelling. Jianshi treats heat in the palm, swollen axilla and elbow spasm, see mania. Diwuhui, Yangfu, Shenmai, Weiyang, Tianchi, Linqi and Xiaxi, listed under fistula, treat axillary swelling. Daling treats elbow spasm and axillary swelling. Linqi treats axillary swelling and chest fullness. Qiuxu and Yangqiao treat axillary swelling, chills and fever, and neck swelling. Shaohai treats axillary scrofula, see scrofula. Chengjin and others treat axillary swelling, see hemorrhoids.

💡 譩譆 BL45、少府 HT8、少海 HT3、支沟 TE6、天池 PC1、胆俞 BL19、委阳 BL39、阳辅 GB38、间使 PC5、地五会 GB42、申脉 BL62、足临泣 GB41、侠溪 GB43、太陵 PC7、丘墟 GB40、阳跷（指申脉）、承筋 BL56。『阳跷』是奇经八脉之一，这里指其交会穴申脉。『尸厥』指突然昏厥如死、脉犹动。『瘰3371』是缺字，应是『瘰疬』，指颈腋部淋巴结结核。承筋在小腿，说它治『掖肿』是《甲乙经》收录的文字，今日少用。这些多是各书条文汇编，今日考试要记主要的：腋下肿取足少阳胆经穴（临泣、丘墟）。

腋下腫痛最不可忽予屢見患瘡癤人腋下或發瘡有至於不可救者可不蚤治之乎

腋下肿痛最不可忽视。我屡次见到患疮疖的人，腋下也生疮，甚至到了无法救治的地步，怎能不及早治疗呢？

Swelling and pain of the axilla must never be neglected. I have often seen people with sores and boils develop sores in the axilla, to the point of being beyond rescue. How can one not treat it early?

💡 这是王执中的临床经验提醒。古代的『疮疖』类似现代的皮肤感染、毛囊炎、痈；感染扩散到腋下淋巴结，可能发展成败血症，在没有抗生素的年代死亡率很高。今天的对应：腋下肿痛伴发热，要看医生，可能需要抗生素或切开引流；若无痛的肿块持续增大，要排除淋巴瘤和乳腺癌转移。『蚤』通『早』。这是少有的、古代观点仍然值得重视的提醒。

腕勞

（标题）腕劳。

(Heading) Wrist taxation.

💡 『劳』在这里指过度使用造成的损伤，近似现代的腕部劳损，如腕管综合征、腱鞘炎（妈妈手）、腕关节慢性劳损。

曲池腕骨等主腕急千見臂痛陽谿療臂腕外側痛不舉明列缺療腕勞上同臂肘痛銅云手腕無力下偏厯療臂3183肘腕酸痛難伸屈外闗療肘腕酸重後谿療肘臂腕重並見手掣通里療肘腕酸重

曲池、腕骨等主治腕部拘急，《千金》，见『臂痛』门。阳溪主治臂腕外侧疼痛、不能抬举，《明堂》。列缺主治腕劳，同上；『臂肘痛』门，《铜人》说（治）手腕无力。下（文）偏历主治臂（缺字）、肘腕酸痛、难以屈伸。外关主治肘腕酸重。后溪主治肘臂腕沉重，并见『手掣』门。通里主治肘腕酸重。

Quchi, Wangu and others treat tense wrist (Qianjin), see arm pain. Yangxi treats pain on the outer arm and wrist with inability to lift (Mingtang). Lieque treats wrist taxation, as above; under arm and elbow pain, the Tongren says it treats weak wrist. Below, Pianli treats arm (a character lost), and aching elbow and wrist with difficulty flexing and extending. Waiguan treats aching, heavy elbow and wrist. Houxi treats heavy elbow, arm and wrist, listed under hand cramps. Tongli treats aching, heavy elbow and wrist.

💡 曲池 LI11、腕骨 SI4、阳溪 LI5、列缺 LU7、偏历 LI6、外关 TE5、后溪 SI3、通里 HT5。『手掣』指手部抽掣。这些穴位多为各条经的络穴或原穴，在腕部附近或上肢远端。今天可用于腕管综合征、腱鞘炎、网球肘；其中阳溪（鼻烟壶处）常用于桡骨茎突狭窄性腱鞘炎。

肘痛肘攣不仁

（标题）肘痛、肘拘挛、麻木不仁。

(Heading) Elbow pain, elbow spasm, and numbness.

💡 对应现代的网球肘、高尔夫球肘、肘管综合征、肱骨外上髁炎等；『不仁』提示神经受累，例如尺神经卡压。

前谷後谿陽谿主肘攣魚際靈道主肘攣柱滿太陵主肘攣腋腫中膂俞譩譆主腋攣曲池主肘中痛見臂曲池腕骨臑㑹支溝肘窌主肘節痹臂酸重腋急痛肘難屈伸中衝等主肘痛見臂痛闗衝主肘疼不能自帶衣閒使主肘内㢘痛曲池三里闗衝中渚陽谷尺澤主肘痛時寒曲池主肘痛見四支厥肩外俞主肘寒見肩背痛天宗治臂肘外後㢘痛銅天髎治肩肘痛見肩背痛肘髎治肘節風痹臂痛不可舉屈伸攣急明下云肘臂酸重麻痹不仁魚際治肘攣支滿靈道見心痛尺澤少海見腋治肘攣見風痹支正内闗見中風陽谿治

💡 前谷 SI2、后溪 SI3、阳溪 LI5、鱼际 LU10、灵道 HT4、太陵 PC7、中膂俞 BL29、譩譆 BL45、曲池 LI11、腕骨 SI4、臑会 TE13、支沟 TE6、肘髎 LI12、中冲 PC9、关冲 TE1、间使 PC5、手三里 LI10、中渚 TE3、阳谷 SI5、尺泽 LU5、肩外俞 SI14、天宗 SI11、天髎 TE15、少海 HT3、支正 SI7、内关 PC6。『肘窌』即肘髎。『节痹』指关节的痹证。肘部疾病通常取曲池、尺泽、小海（此处未列）、天井、手三里等，与今天网球肘的针灸取穴大体一致。『时寒』指时时恶寒，可能是风寒痹的表现。

驚掣肘臂不舉極泉治臂肘厥寒竅隂見喉痹手三里治手臂肘攣不伸後谿治臂肘攣急附分治臂肘不仁見風勞腕骨治偏枯臂肘不得屈伸五里見風勞天井見風痹下㢘治臂肘痛衝陽見口喎曲池治肘中痛魚際療肘攣支滿喉中焦乾渴痙上氣明偏厯見腕三里療肘臂酸重屈伸難下中渚療肘臂酸痛見手掣太淵療肘痛曲池療肘痛屈伸難手不得舉偏風半身不遂捉物不得挽弓不開肘臂偏細孔最療肘臂厥痛屈伸難手不及頭不握支正療肘臂攣難屈伸手不握十指盡痛肘髎療肘臂酸重不可屈伸麻痹不仁天井療肘痛引肩不可屈伸頸項及肩背痛臂痿不仁液門療肘痛不能上下列缺主肘

（阳溪治）惊掣、肘臂不能抬举。极泉主治臂肘厥冷发寒；窍阴见『喉痹』门。手三里主治手臂肘拘挛、不能伸。后溪主治臂肘拘挛急。附分主治臂肘麻木不仁，见『风劳』门。腕骨主治偏枯、臂肘不能屈伸；五里见『风劳』门；天井见『风痹』门。下廉主治臂肘痛；冲阳见『口㖞』门。曲池主治肘中痛。鱼际主治肘拘挛、胀满、喉中干焦、口渴、痉、气上逆，《明堂》；偏历见『腕』门。（手）三里主治肘臂酸重、屈伸困难；下（文）中渚主治肘臂酸痛，见『手掣』门。太渊主治肘痛。曲池主治肘痛、屈伸困难、手不能举、偏风半身不遂、握物不得、挽弓不开、肘臂一侧细瘦。孔最主治肘臂厥痛、屈伸困难、手不能抬到头、不能握。支正主治肘臂拘挛、难以屈伸、手不能握、十指尽痛。肘髎主治肘臂酸重、不可屈伸、麻痹不仁。天井主治肘痛牵引肩、不可屈伸、颈项及肩背痛、臂痿不仁。液门主治肘痛、不能上下。列缺主治肘（接下行）。

(Yangxi treats) fright with cramps and inability to raise the elbow and arm. Jiquan treats cold reversal of arm and elbow; Qiaoyin is listed under throat impediment. 💡 极泉 HT1、窍阴（见喉痹）、手三里 LI10、后溪 SI3、附分 BL41、腕骨 SI4、五里 LI13、天井 TE10、下廉 LI8、冲阳 ST42、曲池 LI11、鱼际 LU10、偏历 LI6、中渚 TE3、太渊 LU9、孔最 LU6、支正 SI7、肘髎 LI12、液门 TE2、列缺 LU7。『偏风半身不遂』指中风后半身不遂，『捉物不得、挽弓不开』形容手握力差、不能做拉弓动作。『痉』指肌肉强直或抽搐。『偏枯』是中风后一侧肢体枯萎瘫痪。『喉中焦干渴』与鱼际（肺经荥穴，属火）清肺热的作用有关。曲池是这一段出现最多的穴位，今天仍是治上肢瘫痪、肘痛、发热、皮肤病的常用穴。

甲乙經云五里在肘上三寸大脈中玉篇説肘云臂節也此臂之下節也宓子賤使書吏書而掣其肘盖其掌節也當以此求之

《甲乙经》说：五里在肘上三寸、大脉之中。《玉篇》解释『肘』说：是臂的关节，这是臂的下节。宓子贱让书吏写字，却拉扯他的肘，大概就是（拉住）他掌握的关节。应当用这样的意思去找（肘部的位置）。

The Jiayijing says Wuli is three cun above the elbow in the great vessel. The Yupian explains 'elbow' as the joint of the arm, that is, the lower joint of the arm. When Mi Zijian had a clerk write while pulling at his elbow, that was probably the joint that controls the palm. One should use this meaning to locate it.

💡 五里（手五里）LI13，《甲乙经》定位在肘上三寸。《资生经》这里考证『肘』的字义，用来确定五里相对于肘关节的位置。宓子贱使书吏的典故出自《吕氏春秋》，讲宓子贱拉住书吏的肘让他写不好字，证明他的肘部对书写的作用。学习要点：古人常用字书、典故来考定穴位位置，今天则以 WHO 标准定位和骨度分寸为准：手五里在曲池上3寸，位于曲池与肩髃连线上。

手麻痹不仁不舉

（标题）手麻痹、麻木不仁、不能抬举。

(Heading) Numb, impeded, insensitive hand with inability to lift.

💡 对应现代：腕管综合征、颈椎病神经根压迫、周围神经病变、糖尿病末梢神经病变、中风后手功能障碍。手麻伴有无力、言语不清、面瘫，须紧急排除中风。

中封治身體不仁銅見3369少商主手不仁千肩貞主手髍小不舉内庭主四厥手足悶列缺主四支厥喜笑曲池支溝臑㑹腕骨肘窌主節痹曲池天井外闗主臂痿不仁白環俞療手足不仁明下見脊曲池主手不舉又主手不可舉重腕急肘中痛難屈伸閒使主手痛陽谿主臂腕外側痛不舉中衝少衝明同勞宫太泉經渠列缺主手

中封主治身体麻木不仁，《铜人》所载，见（缺字）门。少商主治手麻木不仁，《千金》。肩贞主治手（缺字）小、不能抬举。内庭主治四肢厥冷、手足憋闷。列缺主治四肢厥冷、喜笑。曲池、支沟、臑会、腕骨、肘髎主治关节痹。曲池、天井、外关主治臂痿、麻木不仁。白环俞主治手足不仁，《明堂》下见『脊』门。曲池主治手不能举，又主治手不能举重物、腕拘急、肘中痛、难以屈伸。间使主治手痛。阳溪主治臂腕外侧痛、不能抬举。中冲、少冲，《明堂》同；劳宫、太渊、经渠、列缺主治手（接下一行）。

Zhongfeng treats numbness of the body (Tongren; see a lost heading). Shaoshang treats numb hand (Qianjin). Jianzhen treats a hand that is small (a character is lost) and cannot lift. Neiting treats reversal cold of the four limbs and oppression of hands and feet. Lieque treats reversal cold of the four limbs and inappropriate laughter. 💡 中封 LR4、少商 LU11、肩贞 SI9、内庭 ST44、列缺 LU7、曲池 LI11、支沟 TE6、臑会 TE13、腕骨 SI4、肘髎 LI12、天井 TE10、外关 TE5、白环俞 BL30、间使 PC5、阳溪 LI5、中冲 PC9、少冲 HT9、劳宫 PC8、太渊 LU9、经渠 LU8。原文『3369』为缺字。『喜笑』是神志症状，列缺治它在条文中罕见。井穴（少商、中冲、少冲）常用于急救和手指疾患，体现『井主心下满』类说法（《难经》）。白环俞在骶部，说它治手足不仁，是通过膀胱经和督脉的联系推理而来，今天罕用。

掌熱肘中痛勞宫療手掌厚瘡痹銅云手痹手皮白屑起明勞宫治手痹銅附分治肘臂不仁銅見風勞上㢘治手足不仁見偏風肘髎天井療肘臂不仁明下見附痛有貴人手中指攣已而無名指小指亦攣醫為灸肩髀曲池支溝而愈支溝在腕後三寸或灸風疾多有不灸支溝只灸合谷云

（手）掌发热、肘中痛。劳宫主治手掌生厚疮、痹，《铜人》说：手痹、手皮起白屑，《明堂》。劳宫主治手痹，《铜人》。附分主治肘臂麻木不仁，《铜人》，见『风劳』门。上廉主治手足不仁，见『偏风』门。肘髎、天井主治肘臂麻木不仁，《明堂》下见『附痛』。有一位贵人，手中指拘挛，不久无名指和小指也拘挛，医生给他灸肩髀、曲池、支沟而治好。支沟在腕后三寸。有人灸风疾，多不灸支沟，只灸合谷。

(Zhongchong etc. treat) heat in the palm and pain within the elbow. Laogong treats thick sores and impediment of the palm (Tongren says: hand impediment with white scaling skin, as in the Mingtang). Laogong treats hand impediment (Tongren). Fufen treats numb elbow and arm (Tongren; see wind taxation). Shanglian treats numb hands and feet, see hemiplegia. Zhouliao and Tianjing treat numb elbow and arm (Mingtang; see 'fu pain' below). There was a noble whose middle finger became contracted, then the ring and little fingers also contracted; the doctor moxaed the shoulder (Jianyu), Quchi and Zhigou and he recovered. Zhigou lies three cun above the wrist. Many who moxa for wind disease do not moxa Zhigou and moxa only Hegu.

💡 劳宫 PC8、附分 BL41、上廉 LI9、肘髎 LI12、天井 TE10、肩髀（疑指肩髃 LI15 或肩髃与肩髎）、曲池 LI11、支沟 TE6、合谷 LI4。『掌中厚疮、手皮白屑』类似今天的掌跖角化、湿疹、真菌感染。这里记了一个病案：手指挛缩由中指蔓延到无名指、小指，王执中认为灸肩、肘、腕后的穴位而愈。今天这个表现像尺神经病变或掌腱膜挛缩（Dupuytren 挛缩），但古代记载缺少诊断，只能当作历史案例。重点：支沟在腕背横纹上3寸，手少阳经穴，作者提醒不要只灸合谷、不灸支沟，强调多穴配合。

手指攣手掣痛餘論見手麻

（标题）手指拘挛、手抽掣疼痛；其余讨论见『手麻』门。

(Heading) Contracted fingers and hand cramps with pain; for further discussion see 'numb hand'.

💡 对应现代的手指屈肌腱鞘炎（弹响指）、Dupuytren 挛缩、周围神经病变、中风后手指痉挛、腕管综合征。

養老主手不得上下千隂交主手足拘攣太陵主手攣不伸心俞肝俞主筋急手相引見小筋少商治手攣指痛銅少府治掌中熱股腋攣急胷中痛手卷不伸少衝療手卷不得伸明堂上云治手攣不伸引眼痛外闗療肘腕酸重屈伸難十指痛不得握明下銅人云治肘臂不得屈伸五指痛不能握物後谿療肘臂腕重難屈伸五指盡痛不可掣中渚療肘臂痠痛手五指不握盡痛銅人云治咽腫肘臂痛五指不得屈伸腕骨中渚主五指掣不可屈伸大陵主手掣小偏尺澤主掣痛手不可伸治手足指掣痛不可忍灸指端七壯立差腕骨治瘈瘲五指掣銅扁骨療指臂痛明下見臂痛

养老主治手不能上下活动。阴交主治手足拘挛，《千金》。太陵主治手拘挛、不能伸。心俞、肝俞主治筋拘急、手相互牵引，见『小筋』门。少商主治手拘挛、指痛，《铜人》。少府主治掌中热、大腿与腋拘挛急、胸中痛、手蜷缩不能伸。少冲主治手蜷缩不能伸，《明堂》上云：治手拘挛不能伸、牵引眼痛。外关主治肘腕酸重、屈伸困难、十指疼痛不能握，《明堂》下、《铜人》云：治肘臂不能屈伸、五指痛不能握物。后溪主治肘臂腕沉重、难以屈伸、五指尽痛、不可抽掣。中渚主治肘臂酸痛、手五指不能握、都痛，《铜人》云：治咽肿、肘臂痛、五指不能屈伸。腕骨、中渚主治五指抽掣、不能屈伸。大陵主治手抽掣。小偏（疑指小指偏）。尺泽主治抽掣疼痛、手不能伸；治手足指抽掣痛不可忍，灸指端七壮，立即痊愈。腕骨主治瘈疭、五指抽掣，《铜人》。扁骨（肩髃）主治指臂痛，《明堂》下见『臂痛』门。

Yanglao treats inability to move the hand up and down. Yinjiao treats contracture of hands and feet (Qianjin). Daling treats contracted hand that cannot extend. Xinshu and Ganshu treat tense sinews with hands pulling at each other, see small sinews. Shaoshang treats contracted hand and painful fingers (Tongren). Shaofu treats heat in the palm, spasm of thigh and axilla, chest pain, and a curled hand that cannot extend. Shaochong treats a curled hand that cannot extend (the Mingtang above says: treats contracted hand with pain pulling to the eye). Waiguan treats aching, heavy elbow and wrist with difficulty flexing and extending and pain in all ten fingers with inability to grip (the Mingtang below and the Tongren say: treats elbow and arm that cannot flex or extend and five fingers that hurt and cannot grasp things). Houxi treats heavy elbow, arm and wrist with difficulty flexing, pain in all five fingers, and cramps. Zhongzhu treats aching elbow and arm and a hand whose five fingers cannot grip, with pain in all (the Tongren says it treats throat swelling, elbow and arm pain, and five fingers that cannot flex or extend). Wangu and Zhongzhu treat cramps of the five fingers with inability to flex or extend. Daling treats cramps of the hand. Chize treats cramping pain with a hand that cannot extend; for unbearable cramp pain in the fingers and toes, moxa the finger tips seven cones for immediate recovery. Wangu treats tetany and cramps of the five fingers (Tongren). Bian bone (Jianyu) treats pain in fingers and arm, see arm pain in the Mingtang.

💡 养老 SI6、阴交 CV7、太陵 PC7、心俞 BL15、肝俞 BL18、少商 LU11、少府 HT8、少冲 HT9、外关 TE5、后溪 SI3、中渚 TE3、腕骨 SI4、尺泽 LU5、肩髃 LI15（扁骨）。『筋急』与『肝主筋』理论有关：肝俞、心俞（心主血脉，母子关系）用于筋拘急。『瘈疭』指抽搐，『掣』指牵拉抽动。『灸指端七壮』是指在手指尖或脚趾尖灸七壮，是古代急救和止痛的经验法，今天罕用，且灸指端容易烫伤。这段需要区分：局部取穴（外关、后溪、中渚）、经络远端取穴（养老）、与脏腑理论取穴（心俞、肝俞）三类。

手熱手寒手清手心熱

（标题）手热、手寒、手清冷、手心发热。

(Heading) Hot hands, cold hands, cool hands, and heat in the palms.

💡 『清』在古汉语里是『凉、冷』的意思。作者在后文用《礼记》『冬温而夏清』作证。对应现代：手足多汗、手足心热（阴虚）、末梢循环不良、雷诺现象、甲状腺功能异常等。

小兒食時頭痛及五心熱灸譩譆各一壯明下内闗主手中風熱千中衝少衝太泉明下同勞宫經渠列缺主手掌熱肘痛太谿主手足寒至節曲澤主手清逆氣巨闕主手清經渠列缺見偏風少衝見傷寒中衝見心痛閒使見狂太淵治掌中熱閒使治掌中熱見狂中衝治掌熱見傷寒無汗陽陵泉治脚冷見月大都治手足逆冷見腹滿少商治掌熱見指攣豐隆治厥逆見尸厥内庭章門治厥逆行閒治四支冷曲池等肩外俞主肘寒千見肘太谿治手足冷見腹脇痛

小儿吃饭时头痛及五心发热，灸譩譆各一壮，《明堂》下。内关主治手中风热，《千金》。中冲、少冲、太渊，《明堂》下同；劳宫、经渠、列缺主治手掌热、肘痛。太溪主治手足寒冷到关节。曲泽主治手清冷、气逆。巨阙主治手清冷。经渠、列缺见『偏风』门；少冲见『伤寒』门；中冲见『心痛』门；间使见『狂』门。太渊主治掌中热。间使主治掌中热，见『狂』门。中冲主治掌热，见『伤寒』门无汗。阳陵泉主治脚冷，见『月（疑为足）』门。大都主治手足逆冷，见『腹满』门。少商主治掌热，见『指挛』门。丰隆主治厥逆，见『尸厥』门。内庭、章门主治厥逆。行间主治四肢冷。曲池等。肩外俞主治肘寒，《千金》，见『肘』门。太溪主治手足冷，见『腹胁痛』门。

For a child with headache while eating and heat in the five hearts, moxa Yixi one cone on each side (Mingtang below). Neiguan treats wind-heat in the hand (Qianjin). Zhongchong, Shaochong and Taiyuan (Mingtang below the same); Laogong, Jingqu and Lieque treat heat in the palm and elbow pain. Taixi treats cold hands and feet reaching the joints. Quze treats cool hands and counterflow qi. Juque treats cool hands. Jingqu and Lieque are under wind hemiplegia; Shaochong under cold damage; Zhongchong under heart pain; Jianshi under mania. Taiyuan treats heat in the palm. Jianshi treats heat in the palm, see mania. Zhongchong treats hot palms without sweating, see cold damage. Yanglingquan treats cold legs, see leg (heading). Dadu treats reversal cold of hands and feet, see abdominal fullness. Shaoshang treats hot palms, see finger spasm. Fenglong treats reversal counterflow, see corpse reversal. Neiting and Zhangmen treat reversal counterflow. Xingjian treats cold limbs. Quchi and others. Jianwaishu treats cold elbow (Qianjin), see elbow. Taixi treats cold hands and feet, see abdominal and flank pain.

💡 譩譆 BL45、内关 PC6、中冲 PC9、少冲 HT9、太渊 LU9（原作『太泉』）、劳宫 PC8、经渠 LU8、列缺 LU7、太溪 KI3、曲泽 PC3、巨阙 CV14、间使 PC5、阳陵泉 GB34、大都 SP2、少商 LU11、丰隆 ST40、内庭 ST44、章门 LR13、行间 LR2、曲池 LI11、肩外俞 SI14。『五心热』指两手心、两足心和胸中（心胸）发热，是中医阴虚内热的典型表现；原书把小儿食后头痛、五心热归为『伤食』，即消化不良积滞化热。对照现代：小儿消化不良、发热、运动后多汗、甲亢、更年期潮热等，需要具体鉴别。『太泉』避唐讳，即太渊。阳陵泉一条『见月』疑为『见足』之误。

五心之熱小兒傷食證也大人亦然若手足寒清過節證惡可知當早隨證鍼灸之母使至於此極方可清猶寒也禮記言冬温而夏清是已鍼灸法見四支厥

五心发热，是小儿伤食的证候，大人也是这样。如果手足寒凉超过关节，病证的严重性就可想而知，应当及早按证针灸，不要让病发展到那个地步才（治疗）才可以。『清』就是『寒』的意思。《礼记》说『冬温而夏清』，指的就是这个。针灸方法见『四肢厥』门。

Heat in the five hearts is a pattern of food damage in children, and it is the same in adults. If cold in the hands and feet goes beyond the joints, the severity of the pattern can be known; one should treat early by pattern with acupuncture and moxibustion and not let it reach that extreme. 'Qing' means cold. The Liji says 'warm in winter, cool in summer', which means this. For the acupuncture and moxibustion method, see limb reversal.

💡 原文『母使』应为『毋使』，意思是『不要让』。『清犹寒也』是训诂，『冬温夏清』出自《礼记·曲礼》，说子女侍奉父母，冬天要让父母温暖，夏天要让父母凉爽。古人很重视『早治』，这也是《素问》『不治已病治未病』的精神。『手足寒清过节』指寒冷过肘过膝，在古代常与阳气衰微、厥逆相关，需要及早处理；今日若见四肢冰冷伴发绀、意识改变、血压下降等，是急症，要立即就医。『小儿伤食』在今日相当于食积、消化不良，主要靠调整饮食；针灸或推拿可作辅助。

足麻痹不仁

（标题）足麻痹、麻木不仁。

(Heading) Numb and impeded feet with loss of sensation.

💡 对应现代：坐骨神经痛、腰椎间盘突出、糖尿病周围神经病变、末梢血管病、中风后下肢感觉障碍。足麻伴无力、大小便障碍、单侧无力，须立即就诊。

至隂主風寒從足小指起脈痹上下千隂陵泉主足痹痛中都主足濕痹不能行陽輔陽交陽陵泉主髀樞脚骨痹不仁陽閒環跳承筋主脛痹不仁腰俞風府主足不仁膀胱俞太谿次窌主足清不仁大谿次窌膀胱俞主足清不仁腰俞風府明同主足不仁陽闗主脛痹不仁見足雜病浮郗治髀樞不仁銅見足雜病膀胱俞治脚足不仁見腰脚白環俞療手足不仁明下見脊上㢘治手足不仁銅見偏風

至阴主治风寒从足小趾起、脉痹，上下（受累），《千金》。阴陵泉主治足痹痛。中都主治足湿痹、不能行走。阳辅、阳交、阳陵泉主治髀枢、脚骨痹、不仁。阳（关）、环跳、承筋主治胫痹不仁。腰俞、风府主治足不仁。膀胱俞、太溪、次髎主治足冷、不仁。太溪、次髎、膀胱俞主治足冷不仁。腰俞、风府，《明堂》同，主治足不仁。阳关主治胫痹不仁，见『足杂病』门。浮郄主治髀枢不仁，《铜人》，见『足杂病』门。膀胱俞主治脚足不仁，见『腰脚』门。白环俞主治手足不仁，《明堂》下见『脊』门。上廉主治手足不仁，《铜人》，见『偏风』门。

Zhiyin treats wind-cold starting from the little toe with vessel impediment affecting up and down (Qianjin). Yinlingquan treats painful impediment of the foot. Zhongdu treats damp impediment of the foot with inability to walk. Yangfu, Yangjiao and Yanglingquan treat hip pivot and bone impediment of the leg with numbness. Yang(guan), Huantiao and Chengjin treat shin impediment with numbness. Yaoshu and Fengfu treat numb feet. Pangguangshu, Taixi and Ciliao treat cold feet with numbness. Taixi, Ciliao and Pangguangshu treat cold, numb feet. Yaoshu and Fengfu, same in the Mingtang, treat numb feet. Yangguan treats shin impediment with numbness, see miscellaneous foot diseases. Fuxi treats numbness of the hip pivot (Tongren), see miscellaneous foot diseases. Pangguangshu treats numb feet, see lumbar and leg. Baihuanshu treats numb hands and feet, see spine (Mingtang). Shanglian treats numb hands and feet (Tongren), see hemiplegia.

💡 至阴 BL67、阴陵泉 SP9、中都 LR6、阳辅 GB38、阳交 GB35、阳陵泉 GB34、阳关 GB33、环跳 GB30、承筋 BL56、腰俞 GV2、风府 GV16、膀胱俞 BL28、太溪 KI3、次髎 BL32、浮郄 BL38、白环俞 BL30、上廉 LI9（手上廉）。『阳闲』应是『阳关』的另写，原文两处出现。『髀枢』指髋关节（股骨大转子处）。足少阳经、足太阳经、足少阴经是足部麻木、冷痛常取的经脉：太阳经走小腿后侧、少阳经走外侧、少阴经走内侧。上廉是手阳明经的穴位，所以说它治『手足不仁』是通过『偏风』（中风偏瘫）的理论，今天须和一般肢体麻木区分开来。腰俞、风府一个在骶部、一个在枕部，都是督脉穴，督脉总督一身阳气，足麻不仁取督脉有『整体调阳』的意味。

犢鼻髀闗陽陵泉主脚不仁千見腰痛

犊鼻、髀关、阳陵泉主治脚麻木不仁，《千金》，见『腰痛』门。

Dubi, Biguan and Yanglingquan treat numb feet (Qianjin), see lumbar pain.

💡 犊鼻 ST35、髀关 ST31、阳陵泉 GB34。这三个穴位分别在膝、大腿前、小腿外侧，是下肢痹证的常用穴。今天犊鼻（外膝眼）常用于膝关节痛，阳陵泉为筋会，用于筋病和下肢痿痹。

列子載偃師造倡云廢其腎則足不能行是足之不能行盖腎有病也當灸腎俞或一再灸而不效冝灸

《列子》记载偃师造了一个木偶（倡）说，若废掉它的肾，就不能行走。由此可知，足不能行，大概是肾有病。应当灸肾俞，或者灸一次、再灸一次仍然无效，就应当灸（下面这些穴位）。

The Liezi records that the artisan Yan Shi made a puppet and said that if its kidneys were removed, its feet could not walk. So inability of the feet to walk is probably because the kidney is diseased. One should moxa Shenshu; if one or two moxa sessions do not work, one should moxa (the following points).

💡 肾俞 BL23。《列子·汤问》载偃师造木偶，周穆王看了后，偃师说拆掉木偶的心，口不能言；拆掉肝，目不能视；拆掉肾，足不能行。这是文学故事，作者借用来支持『肾主骨、主下肢』的思想（《素问》：肾主骨）。我们学习时要区分：故事不是医学证据，但『肾与下肢、腰膝』的关联是中医脏象的基本内容，在今天的中医教学里表现为『腰膝酸软、下肢无力多为肾虚』。现代医学看足不能行，要考虑神经、肌肉、骨关节、血管等原因，不能一律归肾虚。

環跳風市犢鼻月闗陽陵泉隂陵泉三里絶骨等穴但按畧痠疼即是受病處灸之無不效也

环跳、风市、犊鼻、髀关（原文作『月关』）、阳陵泉、阴陵泉、（足）三里、绝骨等穴，只要按压时稍微有酸痛的，就是病变所在的地方，灸这些地方没有不见效的。

At Huantiao, Fengshi, Dubi, Biguan (written as Yueguan in the text), Yanglingquan, Yinlingquan, Zusanli, Juegu and similar points, wherever pressing produces even slight soreness is the diseased place, and moxa there is never without effect.

💡 环跳 GB30、风市 GB31、犊鼻 ST35、髀关 ST31（原文『月闗』应是『髀关』，月旁与骨旁字形相近）、阳陵泉 GB34、阴陵泉 SP9、足三里 ST36、绝骨即悬钟 GB39。这是王执中的取穴原则：『按之酸疼即是受病处』，即用压痛、酸胀反应来定穴，与阿是穴、『以痛为腧』的思想相通。『无不效也』是夸大的说法，今天不应当成保证。学习要点：下肢痿痹、不能行走时，可以取足三阳经、足三阴经的下肢穴位，再配合按压反应点。若有进行性无力、感觉丧失、大小便异常，应先到医院检查。

足不能行不能立不收

（标题）足不能行走、不能站立、不能收拢（或收缩）。

(Heading) Inability of the feet to walk, inability to stand, and failure to draw in (or contract).

💡 此处是标题行，其下原文缺（卷5此节正文未收录于所给原文）。对应现代：下肢瘫痪、多发性神经炎、脊髓病变、肌营养不良、重症肌无力、格林-巴利综合征等。这类病急性发作、进展迅速的须急诊；古代『痿证』多取阳明经穴（『治痿独取阳明』，出《素问·痿论》）。

三隂交療不能行明下

三阴交可治疗不能行走（《明堂下经》所载）。

Sanyinjiao treats inability to walk (as recorded in the Mingtang Lower Classic).

💡 三阴交 SP6，足三阴经交会穴，健脾、养肝肾，是下肢痿弱的常用穴。原文引用标记：明下＝《明堂下经》，千＝《千金方》，铜＝《铜人腧穴针灸图经》，见某某＝参见本书别处该类条目。今天仍用于下肢无力、神经肌肉疾病的辅助治疗。孕妇慎用。

上㢘治喘息不能行見脇痛

上廉治疗喘息、不能行走（参见胁痛条）。

Shanglian treats panting and breathlessness with inability to walk (see also the hypochondriac pain entry).

💡 上廉即上巨虚 ST37，大肠下合穴，主肠腑病和下肢痿痹。古人认为气喘与步行无力同属气机不畅。现代对照：这类症状可见于心肺功能不全或神经肌肉疾病，需先排除器质性病变，不能只靠针灸。

合陽見腰脊治履步難

合阳治疗行走困难（参见腰脊条）。

Heyang treats difficulty in walking (see also the lumbar and spine entry).

💡 合阳 BL55，属足太阳膀胱经，位于小腿后侧，主腰脊强痛、下肢痿痹。膀胱经循行于腰背和下肢后侧，所以腰腿同病时常取此经。现代可见于腰椎间盘突出引起的下肢放射痛、无力。

天柱行間主足不任身千與銅同

天柱、行间主治足部无力、不能支撑身体（《千金方》与《铜人》所载相同）。

Tianzhu and Xingjian govern the feet being unable to bear the body's weight (the Qianjin and Tongren agree).

💡 天柱 BL10 在颈后，行间 LR2 在足背，属肝经荥穴。一上一下配伍，取『肝主筋』『膀胱经行于项背下肢』之意。今天可作为颈椎相关或肝经病所致下肢无力的参考配穴。

京門主腰痛不能立

京门主治腰痛、不能站立。

Jingmen governs low back pain with inability to stand.

💡 京门 GB25，肾之募穴，位于十二肋端。肾主腰脊，所以取肾募治腰痛。现代对照：可见于腰肌劳损、肾结石等引起的腰痛，肾区痛应先排除泌尿系疾病。

然谷治䯒痠不能久立銅見失情

然谷治疗小腿酸软、不能久站（《铜人》所载；参见『失精』条）。

Rangu treats aching, weak lower legs and inability to stand for long (Tongren; see the seminal emission entry).

💡 然谷 KI2，肾经荥穴。肾主骨、主下肢，肾虚则胫酸立久不支。『失精』指遗精，同属肾虚。「䯒」是小腿（胫）的异体字。现代对照：可见于下肢静脉功能不全、电解质异常、腰骶神经问题，临床需先排除这些原因。

承山治戰慄不能立見腰脚

承山治疗战栗（发抖）、不能站立（参见腰脚条）。

Chengshan treats trembling and inability to stand (see the lumbar and leg entry).

💡 承山 BL57，位于腓肠肌肌腹下，是治小腿转筋、腿软的要穴。现代对照：对应腓肠肌痉挛、坐骨神经痛的小腿症状。

漏谷療不能立明下見足寒熱

漏谷治疗不能站立（《明堂下经》所载；参见足寒热条）。

Lougu treats inability to stand (Mingtang Lower Classic; see the cold and hot feet entry).

💡 漏谷 SP7，属脾经，位于小腿内侧。脾主肌肉四肢，脾虚湿盛则腿软无力、肿胀。可配 SP6、SP9 健脾利湿。

飛3951療體重起坐不能步履不收脚腨酸重戰慄不能久立坐

飞扬治疗身体沉重、起坐和行走困难、足不能收，小腿酸重、战栗，不能久立久坐。

Feiyang treats a heavy body, difficulty rising, sitting and walking, inability to draw in the foot, aching and heavy calves, trembling, and inability to stand or sit for long.

💡 原文『飞3951』即飞扬 BL58（3951为底本缺字“扬”），膀胱经络穴，联络肾经。『腨』指腓肠肌（小腿肚）。治疗思路是疏通膀胱、肾两经的经气。

附陽療不能久立坐不能起見腰脚

跗阳治疗不能久立、坐下后起不来（参见腰脚条）。

Fuyang treats inability to stand for long and difficulty getting up from sitting (see the lumbar and leg entry).

💡 跗阳 BL59，阳跷脉郄穴。阳跷脉主下肢外侧运动和『阳气』的升发，所以起立困难取之。现代对照：与下肢肌力下降、腰骶神经根受压有关。

申脈治䯒寒不能久立坐如在舟車中銅見腰脚

申脉治疗小腿发凉、不能久立，坐着时感觉像在船车里摇晃（《铜人》所载；参见腰脚条）。

Shenmai treats cold lower legs, inability to stand for long, and a sensation of swaying as if sitting in a boat or carriage (Tongren; see the lumbar and leg entry).

💡 申脉 BL62，八脉交会穴，通阳跷脉。『如在舟车中』是眩晕、不稳的描述。现代对照：类似前庭功能异常或下肢本体觉异常的不稳感，应先查眩晕原因。

中都主不能行光明同立

中都主治不能行走；光明与之相同，也主不能站立。

Zhongdu governs inability to walk; Guangming is the same, and also governs inability to stand.

💡 中都 LR6，肝经郄穴。光明 GB37，胆经络穴，肝胆相表里，主筋病，也与目病相关。肝主筋，筋弱则不能立行。

三里主不能立並見足寒熱

足三里主治不能站立（并参见足寒热条）。

Zusanli governs inability to stand (also see the cold and hot feet entry).

💡 足三里 ST36，胃经合穴，强壮保健要穴。『治痿独取阳明』，阳明气血充足则筋骨得养。今天仍广泛用于下肢无力和虚弱状态的辅助治疗。

浮白主足緩不收千

浮白主治足部松弛无力、不能收缩（《千金方》所载）。

Fubai governs slack feet that cannot be drawn in (Qianjin).

💡 浮白 GB10，在头部耳后。这是『头穴治足病』的远端取穴，取胆经上病下治。现代临床少用。

三里衝陽僕參飛3951復溜完骨主足痿失履不收

足三里、冲阳、仆参、飞扬、复溜、完骨，主治足痿、不能穿鞋、足不能收。

Zusanli, Chongyang, Pucan, Feiyang, Fuliu and Wangu govern foot atrophy, inability to wear shoes and feet that will not draw in.

💡 ST36、ST42、BL61、BL58、KI7、GB12。『足痿』即下肢萎软无力，相当于运动神经元病、周围神经病变、脊髓病变等。古人以阳明和肾经为主取穴。现代需先明确病因，针灸为辅助。『飞3951』即飞扬（3951为底本缺字“扬”）。

下㢘主驚痹跗不收見乳廱

下廉主治惊悸、痹证、足背不能收（参见乳痈条）。

Xialian governs fright, impediment and a foot dorsum that cannot be drawn in (see the breast abscess entry).

💡 下廉即下巨虚 ST39，小肠下合穴。『痹』是风寒湿等所致肢体疼痛麻木。乳痈条已见，说明下合穴也被用于乳腺急症。现代对照：乳腺炎应及时就医。

豐隆見四支厥

丰隆（参见四肢厥条）。

Fenglong (see the cold limbs entry).

💡 丰隆 ST40，胃经络穴，祛痰要穴。此处是交叉引用，具体主治在后文四肢厥一节。

脾俞治四支不收銅見腹脹

脾俞治疗四肢不能收摄、无力（《铜人》；参见腹胀条）。

Pishu treats limbs that cannot be gathered and controlled (Tongren; see the abdominal distension entry).

💡 脾俞 BL20。脾主四肢，脾虚则四肢无力、怠惰。现代对照：与疲劳、营养不良、肌无力等表现相关。

支溝等治四支不舉

支沟等穴治疗四肢不能举动。

Zhigou and other points treat inability to lift the limbs.

💡 支沟 TE6，三焦经经穴。『等』表示此处省略了其他穴。四肢不举相当于肢体运动障碍。

曲泉大巨銅同等主四支不收並見四支厥

曲泉、大巨（《铜人》同）等穴，主治四肢不能收摄（并参见四肢厥条）。

Ququan and Daju (Tongren agrees) and others govern limbs that cannot be gathered (also see the cold limbs entry).

💡 曲泉 LR8（肝经合穴），大巨 ST27。肝主筋，阳明主润宗筋，故取此二经。

三里主不能久立見唾血虛損

足三里主治不能久站（参见唾血、虚损条）。

Zusanli governs inability to stand for long (see the hemoptysis and deficiency-consumption entries).

💡 ST36。久立不支与虚损相关，所以交叉引用虚损条。这反映中医『虚则补之』的思路：不能久立常是整体虚弱的表现。

足寒熱脛寒又見足雜病

（标题）足部寒热、小腿发寒，另见『足杂病』。

(Heading) Cold and heat in the feet, cold shins; also see the 'Miscellaneous foot disorders' section.

💡 本节讲足部或寒或热，以及小腿寒。「胫」即胫，指小腿。『又见』是交叉引用。

至隂主風寒從足小指起脈痹上下

至阴主治风寒之邪从足小趾开始、经脉痹阻、上下串痛。

Zhiyin governs wind-cold starting from the little toe, with impeded channels and pain moving up and down.

💡 至阴 BL67，膀胱经井穴，位于小趾外侧。井穴是经气始发处，邪从末端入则取井穴。至阴今天最有名的用途是矫正胎位，孕期操作须由专业人员执行。

腎俞京骨然谷主足寒

肾俞、京骨、然谷，主治足寒。

Shenshu, Jinggu and Rangu govern cold feet.

💡 BL23、BL64、KI2。足寒多为肾阳不足、温煦不够。现代对照：可见于末梢循环差、甲状腺功能减退、周围血管病等。

隂市主膝上伏兎中寒

阴市主治膝上、伏兔部位内部发寒。

Yinshi governs cold inside the thigh above the knee, around Futu.

💡 阴市 ST33，在大腿前侧，伏兔 ST32 附近。主寒湿痹阻于股膝。

行間主厥足下熱

行间主治厥证、足心发热。

Xingjian governs syncope-type reversal and heat in the soles.

💡 行间 LR2，肝经荥穴，泻肝火。『厥』在古书中有昏厥、四肢逆冷等多义。足下热多是阴虚或肝火，今天可参考更年期、甲亢等引起的手足心热。

中都主足下熱脛寒不能久立濕痹不能行

中都主治足心发热、小腿发寒、不能久立，以及湿痹导致的不能行走。

Zhongdu governs hot soles, cold shins, inability to stand for long, and damp impediment preventing walking.

💡 LR6。热与寒同见，说明是上下寒热错杂，属肝经病变。『湿痹』即湿邪偏盛的痹证，肢体沉重、疼痛。

三里條口承山承筋主足下熱不能久立

足三里、条口、承山、承筋，主治足心发热、不能久立。

Zusanli, Tiaokou, Chengshan and Chengjin govern hot soles and inability to stand for long.

💡 ST36、ST38、BL57、BL56。条口是治肩周炎的名穴，原书这里用于下肢。足下热可见于糖尿病周围神经病变、烧灼足综合征等，今天需要检查原因。

委中治足熱厥逆滿取其經血立愈銅

委中治疗足部热厥、气逆胀满：取该经脉的血（刺络放血），立即痊愈（《铜人》所载）。

Weizhong treats heat reversal of the feet with counterflow fullness: let blood from the channel and the patient recovers at once (Tongren).

💡 委中 BL40，即『血郄』，放血是传统疗法。现代对照：刺络放血有感染、出血风险，必须无菌操作，凝血功能异常、服抗凝药者禁用。『立愈』是古书夸饰，不能照字面理解。

涌泉治足下熱喘滿乃熱厥也齊王患此鍼之愈

涌泉治疗足心热、气喘胀满，这是『热厥』，齐王患此病，针刺后痊愈。

Yongquan treats hot soles with panting and fullness, which is 'heat reversal'; the King of Qi had this and was cured by needling.

💡 涌泉 KI1，肾经井穴。热厥指邪热内盛、四肢反而厥冷或手足心热。这里引用了淳于意（太仓公）治齐王的医案，见《史记·扁鹊仓公列传》。涌泉今天多用于退虚热、引火归元、治失眠。

至隂治下熱
